(3) with_trashed() lifting more than the soft-delete filter: Builder::lift_soft_deletes (framework/src/eloquent/builder.rs:2788) disables only the "soft_deletes" scope; bind_002_with_trashed_lifts_only_the_soft_delete_filter (trashed.rs:356) asserts another tenant's rows, deleted or not, stay 404 on a with_trashed() route. (4) A #[model(connection = "x")] model binding from the default connection: M::query() carries the model's connection; bind_002_a_model_binds_through_its_own_connection (keys.rs:270) creates ky_archives only on the registered ky_archive connection and asserts 200. (5) A malformed value answering anything but 404 in any binding form: RouteColumn::parse (route_binding.rs:192-200) and the Ok(None) at route_binding.rs:716-718 for the generated lookup, the same parse in __route_child_lookup (route_binding.rs:811-813) for a scoped child, the parse in bare_lookup (route_binding.rs:619-621) for the bare form, and bind_request and miss (framework/src/routing/binding.rs:1232-1259, 1218-1227), which turn every None, an Option<T> argument's included, into the model's 404. bind_002_a_malformed_value_answers_the_404_a_missing_row_does (keys.rs:149) covers the type form (/posts/7abc), RouteParam (/wrapped/7abc) and the bare form (/bare/7abc); bind_002_a_malformed_value_answers_404_for_a_scoped_child_and_an_optional_argument (keys.rs:197) sends `1x` to a scoped child without a field (the scope_bindings route at keys.rs:142-143) and with one (`{book:id}`), and `7abc` to an Option<KyPost> argument, asserting 404 with the missing-row body for each, so an Option that read a malformed value as absent (200 "none") or a child lookup that answered otherwise fails; bind_002_a_malformed_key_is_the_same_miss_as_a_missing_row (regressions.rs:123) covers from_route_param, and bind_003_the_bare_form_binds_an_entity_no_model_defines (regressions.rs:168) a hand-written entity (/notes/1x). This closes the draft gap; the scoped child and Option forms run on SQLite only. (6) A malformed unique_id key answering 500 on Postgres: column_entries adds with_format to a unique_id model's primary key (suprnova-macros/src/model/route_binding.rs:158-189) and RouteColumn::parse checks UniqueIdKind::is_valid first; bind_002_postgres_a_malformed_unique_id_key_answers_404 (keys.rs:320) is #[ignore] (keys.rs:319) but selected by --run-ignored all, reads PG_TEST_URL, which the script exports to a fresh database, and panics (fails, never skips) without it; it covers a uuid column (/tokens/not-a-uuid) and a bigint key (/posts/7abc). bind_002_a_malformed_unique_id_key_answers_404 (keys.rs:252) covers SQLite. (7) The two 404 bodies differing: keys.rs:149 now compares the malformed and missing JSON bodies whole, request_id removed, for the type, RouteParam and bare forms; keys.rs:197 does the same for both scoped children (and their unowned row) and the Option argument; regressions.rs:168 compares the message of both for a hand-written entity. This closes the draft gap. The Postgres test compares only messages. (8) A body containing the value: every malformed request in keys.rs:149, keys.rs:197, keys.rs:252 and regressions.rs:168 asserts the value (`7abc`, `1x`, `not-a-uuid`) is absent from the body; the 404 is FrameworkError::model_not_found(info.name()) (binding.rs:1224-1226), which takes no value. The Postgres test does not check the body for the value. There is no MySQL test of any clause (route_binding has no MySQL variant and the script sets no MYSQL_TEST_URL); the parse runs before the query, so the MySQL case is engine independent by construction only. All tests named are selected by `^route_binding::`; only the Postgres one is #[ignore], and --run-ignored all runs it.
requirement BIND-002 Q4 observed: No code for this requirement changed since 6ee2773b2. The change turned every malformed binding key from FrameworkError::param_parse (400, echoing the value) into a 404 and removed key_type_name and its StoredU64 naming from framework/src/database/route_binding.rs; framework/tests/schema/unsigned_keys.rs (route_binding_finds_a_u64_key at :157, with its SQLite, Postgres and MySQL callers) was rewritten for the 404, but it lives in the `schema` binary, which this mechanism does not run, so u64 keys and the MySQL top-of-range key are not exercised by it. The ULID and uuid_v4 format checks (UniqueIdKind::is_valid, framework/src/eloquent/unique_id.rs:77-82, is_valid_ulid at :198) are never reached through a route; only unique_id = "uuid" (UuidV7) is. The --lib unit test a_unique_id_column_refuses_a_malformed_value (framework/src/database/route_binding.rs:923) is not run by the mechanism. The bare form keeps reading the default connection with no global scope (documented at route_binding.rs:529-534, bare_lookup at :599-636); no test asserts it ignores a model's connection or scope. On Postgres only the type form is driven (keys.rs:320); RouteParam, the bare form, scoped children, Option arguments and binders are SQLite only, and no binding form runs on MySQL. The lookup filters on a table-qualified column, `format!("{}.{}", M::TABLE, column.name())` (route_binding.rs:724, and :819 for a child), on every engine. manual/routing.md's "Why Suprnova diverges" text for strict parsing is checked by the BIND-014 scans alone.
requirement BIND-003 Q3 observed: (1) A handler written with RouteParam<User> stopping compiling or binding differently: RouteParam<T> delegates to T (framework/src/database/route_binding.rs:424-486) and the handler macro reads the parameter named by the single binding of `user: RouteParam<T>` or `RouteParam(user): RouteParam<T>` (suprnova-macros/src/handler.rs:752-773, bound_by_spelling at :568); compiled and driven by bind_003_route_param_still_hides_a_trashed_row (framework/tests/routing/route_binding/regressions.rs:53), bind_003_route_param_answers_model_not_found_for_a_missing_id (:114), bind_003_a_destructured_route_param_binds_the_parameter_named_by_its_binding (:328, including `RouteParam(mut user)`), bind_003_a_destructured_route_param_answers_404_for_a_missing_row (:341) and bind_003_a_plain_route_param_binds_as_before (:348), and by the macro unit test handler::tests::bind_003_existing_binding_forms_still_bind_by_their_binding (handler.rs:977). (2) The bare user::Model form: the blanket `impl<M, E> RouteBinding for M` with bare_lookup by primary key through EntityExt::find_by_pk (route_binding.rs:535-636); bind_003_the_bare_form_still_binds_by_primary_key (regressions.rs:78), the /bare routes of bind_002_a_malformed_value_answers_the_404_a_missing_row_does (keys.rs:149, body "ky_post not found"), and now bind_003_the_bare_form_binds_an_entity_no_model_defines (regressions.rs:168), which declares a hand-written DeriveEntityModel entity `rg_note` with `impl EntityExt` and no #[model], passes prepare_bindings, binds /notes/1 by primary key, binds a row with a set deleted_at on the plain and the with_trashed() route (no #[model] covers the table), answers "rg_note not found" for a missing and a malformed key, and checks from_route_param, route_key_name and route_key on the trait; a bare form that stopped compiling for such an entity, looked it up otherwise, or treated its deleted_at as a soft delete would fail. This closes the draft gap. (3) #[authorize("show", id)] over id: i64: authorize_checks (handler.rs:674-748) accepts a Path target and the check runs after the path pass; bind_015_a_primitive_authorize_target_still_compiles (suprnova-macros/tests/route_binding_build.rs:304, authorize-primitive fixture), bind_003_a_primitive_authorize_target_checks_the_path_value (regressions.rs:395, 200 for 7 and 403 for 8) and handler::tests::bind_015_a_primitive_authorize_target_checks_the_path_value (handler.rs:1347). The five listed changes are each asserted: malformed 404 (keys.rs:149, keys.rs:197), binding before the body (bind_015_a_missing_row_answers_404_before_the_body_is_validated, handler_form.rs:158, and bind_003_a_missing_row_answers_404_before_an_associated_handler_reads_the_body, associated.rs:120), the bare form and with_trashed (bind_008_the_bare_form_follows_with_trashed, trashed.rs:135), undeclared parameters (bind_013_a_bound_argument_for_an_undeclared_parameter_is_refused, bind_013_a_route_param_or_bare_argument_for_an_undeclared_parameter_is_refused and bind_013_a_primitive_for_an_undeclared_parameter_is_refused, startup.rs:179, 200, 229). (4) A #[handler(Self = Posts)] function failing to compile: parse_handler_args and handler_site (handler.rs:418-522) with framework/src/routing/handler_site.rs; bind_003_associated_handlers_take_every_binding_form (route_binding_build.rs:259) compiles the impl-handlers fixture (every binding form, #[authorize] above and below, sync, generic, nested module, get! and group registration), and bind_003_a_handler_inside_an_impl_block_without_its_type_fails_the_build (route_binding_build.rs:269) runs trybuild over suprnova-macros/tests/ui/route-binding/*.rs (no Self, and Self naming another type), comparing the .stderr files; handler::tests::bind_003_self_names_the_type_the_record_is_keyed_by (:1105), bind_003_self_combines_with_every_argument_form_and_authorize (:1134), bind_003_an_argument_that_does_not_start_with_self_is_ignored_as_before (:1227), bind_003_a_malformed_self_argument_is_a_compile_error (:1244) and bind_003_self_must_name_a_type_other_than_self (:1263) check the expansion. (5) Such a function escaping the startup checks: record_items keys the record by `<Self>::fn` (handler.rs:589-653); bind_003_an_associated_handler_reading_an_undeclared_parameter_is_refused (associated.rs:134), bind_003_an_associated_handler_refused_at_startup_stops_the_boot (associated.rs:147, Server::from_config inside catch_unwind, asserting the route and `id`), bind_003_an_associated_handler_refused_at_startup_refuses_handle_request (associated.rs:158) and bind_003_the_other_startup_checks_see_an_associated_handler (associated.rs:172, BIND-004, BIND-006, BIND-007 and BIND-015 refusals). All are selected by `^route_binding::`, the unfiltered route_binding_build binary or `^handler::tests::`; none is ignored or gated.
requirement BIND-003 Q4 observed: No code for this requirement changed since 6ee2773b2. The two old regression files framework/tests/routing/route_binding_route_param_scoped.rs and route_param_destructured.rs were deleted and folded into framework/tests/routing/route_binding/regressions.rs. Behaviour the change touched that no check covers: a primitive path value with a malformed segment still answers through FromParam and FrameworkError::param (HandlerInput::path, framework/src/routing/binding.rs:316-322), which no test asserts; a handler that has no plan (a generic #[handler], whose record is skipped at suprnova-macros/src/handler.rs:258) binds its non-generic RouteBinding arguments through the fallback in HandlerInput::optional_bound (binding.rs:377-385), which ignores binding fields, with_trashed(), missing() and router binders, and no test drives that path (handler_form.rs:220 and :236 drive a generic handler, but with no bound argument outside its generic one); the bare form on Postgres or MySQL; a `#[handler]` in an `impl` of a generic type; the sync-handler branch with Self only in the macro expansion test. The dogfood app and the scaffold templates use no binding form, so no app test exercises the old forms end to end. manual/controllers.md, manual/authorization.md and the "Older binding forms" section of manual/routing.md were rewritten and are checked only by the BIND-014 scans.
requirement BIND-004 Q3 observed: (1) `{post:slug}` registering `post:slug`, or `{post:slug?}` anything but optional `post`: split_placeholder and placeholders (framework/src/routing/binding.rs:721-755) read the name, field and marker, and insert_every_form (framework/src/routing/router.rs:772-787) gives the matcher without_binding_fields (binding.rs:757-785); bind_004_a_binding_field_registers_the_parameter_by_its_name (framework/tests/routing/route_binding/startup.rs:97) asserts req.param("post"), where_alpha("post") and route("su.posts.show") use `post`, and bind_004_an_optional_binding_field_registers_an_optional_parameter (startup.rs:129) asserts /maybe and /maybe/hello. (2) The binding ignoring the field: plan_route stores the field (binding.rs:1123-1127) and lookup passes it (binding.rs:1275-1293); startup.rs:114 asserts /posts/1 binds the row whose slug is "1". (3) A field naming no column, or an unparseable column, starting the application: plan_route checks the field against RouteBindingInfo::columns (binding.rs:1069-1093) built from route_columns, which #[model] emits (suprnova-macros/src/model/route_binding.rs:106-110, 158-189); bind_004_a_field_that_is_not_a_column_is_refused_naming_route_parameter_and_field (startup.rs:147) and bind_004_a_field_whose_type_cannot_be_parsed_from_a_segment_is_refused (startup.rs:156, the `meta` column). A field on a type that records no columns reaching its lookup unchecked is bind_004_a_field_on_a_type_without_columns_reaches_its_lookup_unchecked (startup.rs:166). (4) A non-generic #[handler] route escaping the checks, including through handle_request: record_items submits a HandlerRecord keyed by the function's type (suprnova-macros/src/handler.rs:589-653), record_of (binding.rs:106-118) is read at each registration site, and handle_request calls prepare_bindings before matching (framework/src/server.rs:1062). bind_004_every_registration_site_is_checked (startup.rs:256) refuses a handler reading the undeclared `id` through Router get, post, put, patch, delete, head, options, any and methods (router.rs:1480-1809), and now through every fluent GroupBuilder verb, get, any, post, put, patch, delete, head, options, methods and try_methods (framework/src/routing/group.rs:276-473), plus get! and any! (registered through the Router verbs), group! and nested group! with get! inside (macros.rs:1566), fallback! (macros.rs:1035) and the resource! function form (framework/src/routing/resource.rs:682); a site that lost its record would let the router through and the test fails with "the undeclared `id` was not refused". This closes the draft gap for the fluent verbs. One site is still not driven: an any! inside group!, whose record is taken at macros.rs:1610 (into_group_any_route) and noted at macros.rs:1525; no route_binding test puts any! in a group!. bind_004_the_checks_run_for_a_router_driven_through_handle_request (startup.rs:356) and bind_004_a_fallback_beside_a_refused_route_answers_the_refusal (startup.rs:588) assert 500 through handle_request; handler::tests::bind_004_the_handler_records_every_argument (handler.rs:1070) and bind_004_a_generic_handler_has_no_record (handler.rs:1094) check the record. (5) A refusal panicking: prepare_bindings returns Err (router.rs:1191, build_plans at binding.rs:876-944). bind_004_a_refusal_is_an_error_from_the_boot_path (startup.rs:391) runs each boot path under catch_unwind through refusal_of (startup.rs:377), which fails on a panic and on a boot that succeeds, and asserts an Err naming `GET /users/{user}` and `post` from Server::from_config (server.rs:145-150), Server::try_from_config_with_routes (server.rs:159), Server::try_from_config_with_routes_async (server.rs:189, finish_boot at server.rs:213-219, the constructor Application serves through, framework/src/app/mod.rs:106) and Server::run's own check (server.rs:377-382, port 0, a 30 s timeout counting as a boot that succeeded). bind_004_a_refusal_stops_the_application_boot_without_a_panic (startup.rs:483) starts the test binary again on bind_004_the_application_boot_child (startup.rs:453), which runs an Application with the refused router through run_with_args(["app", "serve", "--no-migrate"]) and exits 1 only when an Err comes back; the parent asserts exit 1 within 60 s, no "panicked" on stderr, and the returned error naming the route and `post`. This closes the draft gap, except that Application::run itself (framework/src/app/mod.rs:1083-1103) goes through run_cli_or_exit (app/mod.rs:1165-1168), which runs the same run_cli with Failures::Print and exits 1 on an Err; no test runs that path, and its exit is argued by construction. (6) A field refusal not naming route, parameter and field: the messages at binding.rs:1075-1092 carry all three; the not-a-column test (startup.rs:147) and now the unparseable-column test (startup.rs:156) assert `GET /posts/{post:...}`, "parameter `post`" and the field. This closes the draft gap. The child test bind_004_the_application_boot_child returns at once unless BIND_APPLICATION_BOOT_CHILD is set, so it passes on its own run. All named tests are selected by `^route_binding::` or `^handler::tests::`; none is ignored or cfg-gated. Since 37741ac5a, bind_004_every_registration_site_is_checked also drives `any!` inside `group!` (record at framework/src/routing/macros.rs:1610), so that site is no longer the gap listed in review-q34-final-gaps.md.
requirement BIND-004 Q4 observed: No routing code changed since 6ee2773b2. Changed and unchecked by the mechanism: the in-source unit tests placeholders_read_names_fields_and_optional_markers, binding_fields_are_left_out_for_the_matcher and binder_keys_read_a_dash_as_an_underscore (framework/src/routing/binding.rs:1314, 1339, 1356) are suprnova --lib tests, which the mechanism does not run. URL filling for route() now splits fields from names (placeholder_name, placeholder_value and optional_values, framework/src/routing/router.rs:376-430), checked here only through the route() call in startup.rs:122-125 (and under BIND-012 in urls.rs); constraints keyed by the bare name are checked only through where_alpha in startup.rs:97. Every registration site was rewired to carry a record; all are now driven by bind_004_every_registration_site_is_checked except an any! inside group! (macros.rs:1610), and the controller form of a resource carries no record (resources.rs:282 expects prepare_bindings to pass). Application::run and run_cli_or_exit (framework/src/app/mod.rs:1083-1103, 1165-1168) are not run; the test drives run_with_args. The handle_request tests (startup.rs:356, 576, 588, associated.rs:158) assert status 500 and not the body. The TypeScript route generator's `{name:column}` reading (suprnova-cli/src/commands/generate_routes.rs) is covered under BIND-015 by the ts-routes fixture.
requirement BIND-005 Q3 observed: (1) A model with route_key = "slug" binding by its primary key: suprnova-macros/src/model/route_binding.rs:23-27 takes the attribute as the route key and emits it as route_key_name and model_route_key_name (:46-48, :112-114), and resolve_model_route_binding resolves by it when the route names no field (framework/src/database/route_binding.rs:709); bind_005_a_route_key_binds_by_its_column (framework/tests/routing/route_binding/keys.rs:303) asserts /pages/about binds, /pages/1 binds the page whose slug is "1" rather than id 1, and /pages/2 is 404, and bind_001_from_route_param_resolves_by_the_route_key (contract.rs:64) asserts the same through from_route_param and RouteParam; bind_001_route_param_resolves_as_its_type_does (contract.rs:101) also asserts RouteParam<CtEssay>::route_key_name equals CtEssay's. (2) An unknown column or an injected field compiling: the parse step (suprnova-macros/src/model/parse.rs:622-644) refuses a route_key that is `__eager`, `__pivot` or no named field, with "`route_key = \"{column}\"` names no column of `{struct_name}`"; bind_005_a_route_key_that_is_no_column_fails_the_build (suprnova-macros/tests/route_binding_build.rs:275) cargo-checks the route-key-unknown fixture and asserts the error contains `route_key = "slug"` and "names no column of `Post`", and the route-key-injected fixture with `route_key = "__eager"` and "names no column"; a model that compiled would fail assert_rejected's not-success check. The `__pivot` case is in the code but no fixture uses it; the falsifier names `__eager` as its example. The tests are selected (the `^route_binding::` filter for keys.rs and contract.rs, the unfiltered route_binding_build binary) and none is ignored.
requirement BIND-005 Q4 observed: No code for this requirement changed since 6ee2773b2. The route key also drives route() and try_route() through RouteBinding::route_key (suprnova-macros/src/model/route_binding.rs:50-52, covered under BIND-012 in urls.rs), RouteParam's delegation, and Router::model, which looks a value up with resolve_route_binding(value, None) (framework/src/routing/binding.rs:669-697); no Router::model test uses a model with a route key (CuUser, TrPost and TrProbe bind by id or by hand). A route_key naming an existing field whose type has no path parser (a JSON or byte column) compiles, and every lookup by it then returns Ok(None), a 404, because RouteColumn::parse returns None (framework/src/database/route_binding.rs:192-200, 716-718); the startup check at binding.rs:1069-1093 inspects only `{name:field}` binding fields, never the route key, and no test covers it. On a #[model(custom_route_binding)] model the attribute still sets ModelRouteBinding::model_route_key_name while the hand-written RouteBinding::route_key_name decides lookups, which no test pins. The check compares the attribute with the Rust field identifier, so a field whose column name differs from its identifier is not distinguished; no test covers that. The manual (manual/routing.md "Binding by another column", manual/eloquent.md) and the feature-map vocabulary for route_key (commit 5bf54ce69) are not compiled or checked beyond the BIND-014 scans.
requirement BIND-006 Q3 observed: (1) `/users/1/posts/{post:slug}` binding another user's post: plan_route scopes a child that has a field (framework/src/routing/binding.rs:1102-1105) and lookup finds it through the parent's child lookup (binding.rs:1276-1291), which #[model] emits as one arm per relation through __route_child_lookup (suprnova-macros/src/model/route_binding.rs:193-221; framework/src/database/route_binding.rs:781-822); bind_006_a_child_with_a_binding_field_is_found_through_its_parent (framework/tests/routing/route_binding/scoped.rs:121) asserts 404 for Grace's post under user 1; keys.rs:197 also asserts an unowned book answers the missing-row body. (2) `{post}` under scope_bindings(): bind_006_scope_bindings_scopes_a_child_without_a_field (scoped.rs:141, RouteBuilder::scope_bindings at framework/src/routing/router.rs:2582) and bind_006_a_group_scopes_its_routes (scoped.rs:162, the group! macro's GroupDef::scope_bindings, framework/src/routing/macros.rs:1215). Not driven: the fluent GroupBuilder::scope_bindings (framework/src/routing/group.rs:132), a get!(...).scope_bindings() (macros.rs:226) and the MultiMethodRouteBuilder form (router.rs:3000), each a separate setter. (3) without_scoped_bindings() still scoping: options.scoped != Some(false) at binding.rs:1103; bind_006_without_scoped_bindings_does_not_scope (scoped.rs:171) on a RouteBuilder route (router.rs:2592); the GroupBuilder (group.rs:139), group! (macros.rs:1222), get! (macros.rs:233) and MultiMethodRouteBuilder (router.rs:3012) forms are not driven. (4) A child bound by bind() being scoped: `binder.is_none()` at binding.rs:1102 and the binder branch at binding.rs:1269-1274; bind_006_a_child_bound_by_bind_is_not_scoped (scoped.rs:185). (5) Belongs-to-many or has-many-through failing to bind or binding an unowned row: RouteChildRelation for BelongsToMany (framework/src/eloquent/relations/belongs_to_many.rs:1528-1574) and HasManyThrough (framework/src/eloquent/relations/through.rs:539-570) narrow the child's query with owned_through_table (framework/src/eloquent/relations/mod.rs:566-591); bind_006_scoping_through_a_belongs_to_many_relation (scoped.rs:219) and bind_006_scoping_through_a_has_many_through_relation (scoped.rs:236) assert the owned row binds and unowned rows answer 404, on SQLite only. (6) A missing, wrongly typed or MorphTo relation found at request time: check_child_relation (binding.rs:1146-1196) runs from build_plans at startup; bind_006_a_relation_the_parent_does_not_declare_is_refused_at_startup (scoped.rs:253), bind_006_a_relation_of_another_type_is_refused_at_startup (scoped.rs:267) and bind_006_a_morph_to_relation_is_refused_at_startup (scoped.rs:283) call prepare_bindings through the refusal helper (framework/tests/routing/route_binding/mod.rs:139), which fails when the router is accepted. (7) The refusal not naming route, parent and relation: the messages at binding.rs:1156-1191 carry all three; the missing-relation test asserts `GET /posts/{post}/tags/{tag:slug}`, `ScPost` and `tags`, and now the wrong-type test asserts `GET /users/{user}/notes/{note:slug}`, `ScUser` (the parent), `notes` and ScComment, and the MorphTo test `GET /comments/{comment}/commentables/{commentable:slug}`, `ScComment` (the parent), `commentables` and MorphTo. This closes the draft gap. bind_003_the_other_startup_checks_see_an_associated_handler (associated.rs:172) repeats the missing-relation refusal for an associated handler (parent and relation). The parent rule itself (the parameter immediately before, when bound) is asserted by bind_006_a_child_without_a_bound_parent_is_not_scoped (scoped.rs:202). All named tests are selected by `^route_binding::`; none is ignored or gated. Since 37741ac5a, framework/tests/routing/route_binding/scoped.rs bind_006_every_scope_bindings_setter_scopes_a_child and bind_006_every_without_scoped_bindings_setter_unscopes_a_child drive every setter site (Router::group, GroupBuilder at group.rs:133 and :140, group!, get!, any!, Router::any and Router::methods at router.rs:3005 and :3017) with a control route; setting any one site's scoping to None fails them.
requirement BIND-006 Q4 observed: No code for this requirement changed since 6ee2773b2. Child lookups were added for every single-target relation kind, but routes drive only HasMany, BelongsToMany and HasManyThrough: the RouteChildRelation impls for HasOne (framework/src/eloquent/relations/has_one.rs:277), BelongsTo (belongs_to.rs:302), MorphMany and MorphOne (morph.rs:859, 884), HasOneThrough (through.rs:574), MorphToMany and MorphedByMany (morph_to_many.rs:1506, 1561) have no route test. owned_through_table builds a raw `IN (SELECT ...)` term with `?` placeholders that the builder rewrites per engine; the mechanism runs it on SQLite only, never on Postgres or MySQL, and the intermediate soft-delete guard a HasManyThrough child lookup adds (__soft_delete_guard, through.rs:566, defined at relations/mod.rs:325) has no test. ChildBindings::Custom, the path for a parent that resolves its own children and is therefore not refused at startup (framework/src/database/route_binding.rs:212-226, binding.rs:1155), has no test. When an optional parent parameter is absent and the child is present, lookup falls back to an unscoped lookup (binding.rs:1277-1293), untested. The scope_bindings and without_scoped_bindings setters other than RouteBuilder's and group!'s scope_bindings are not driven (group.rs:132, 139; macros.rs:226, 233, 1222; router.rs:3000, 3012). The --lib unit test child_relation_is_the_plural_of_the_parameter (route_binding.rs:945) is not run by the mechanism. manual/routing.md "Scoped bindings" and manual/eloquent-relationships.md changed and are checked only by the BIND-014 scans.
requirement BIND-007 Q3 observed: (1) A model's own RouteBinding not being used: #[model(custom_route_binding)] omits the generated impl (suprnova-macros/src/model/route_binding.rs:40-41) and the router calls the type's resolve through BoundArg (framework/src/routing/binding.rs:227-255); bind_007_a_model_can_replace_its_binding (framework/tests/routing/route_binding/custom.rs:135) asserts /tags/RUST binds through CuTag's case-folding lookup, which calls the still-public resolve_model_route_binding, and /tags/1 is 404. (2) A non-model type not binding: bind_007_a_type_that_is_no_model_binds_the_same_way (custom.rs:143) binds CuRegion and asserts the 404 body "CuRegion not found". (3) A bind() resolver losing to the type's binding: lookup tries the binder first (binding.rs:1269-1274); bind_007_bind_wins_over_the_type_and_covers_routes_registered_before_it (custom.rs:157) asserts /users/grace binds by name and /users/1 is 404. (4) A binder registered after a route not covering it: binders live router-wide in RouterBindings::add_binder (binding.rs:845-849) and are applied when the plans are built at the first check (build_plans, binding.rs:876-944), so order does not matter; the same test registers bind() after the route, and bind_007_a_binder_name_reads_a_dash_as_an_underscore and bind_007_model_calls_its_fallback_for_a_missing_row register before it; bind_007_a_model_binder_of_another_type_is_refused (custom.rs:227) is refused with model() registered before and after the route. (5) bind("user-id") not covering {user_id}: binder_key (binding.rs:699-701); bind_007_a_binder_name_reads_a_dash_as_an_underscore (custom.rs:179). (6) model() with a fallback answering 404 for a missing row: Binder::model calls the fallback when the lookup finds nothing (binding.rs:669-697); bind_007_model_calls_its_fallback_for_a_missing_row (custom.rs:194) asserts 200 "user guest 99". (7) A resolver returning another type than the handler argument starting: the type_id comparison in plan_route (binding.rs:1059-1067), against TypeId::of::<R> for bind() (binding.rs:660) and RouteBindingInfo::type_id for model() (binding.rs:692); bind_007_a_resolver_of_another_type_is_refused (custom.rs:214) asserts the bind() refusal names the route, String and CuUser, bind_003_the_other_startup_checks_see_an_associated_handler (associated.rs:172) repeats it for an associated handler, and now bind_007_a_model_binder_of_another_type_is_refused (custom.rs:227) registers model::<CuTag> for a `user: CuUser` argument and asserts the refusal names `GET /users/{user}`, CuTag and `user: CuUser`, before and after the route, while model::<CuUser> is accepted, so a model() type check that compared the wrong id or none fails. This closes the draft gap. (8) A binder for a parameter read as a primitive path value or a form request starting: check_handler (binding.rs:984-996); bind_007_a_binder_for_a_path_value_or_a_form_request_is_refused (custom.rs:261) asserts the refusals name `user: i64` and `user: CuForm`. All tests are selected by `^route_binding::`; none is ignored or gated.
requirement BIND-007 Q4 observed: No code for this requirement changed since 6ee2773b2. Unchecked behaviour the change introduced: the MatchedRoute a bind() resolver receives (framework/src/routing/binding.rs:604-630, built in bind_request at :1234-1238) is checked only through pattern() (custom.rs:163); method(), name() and param() are not; Binder::bind discards the route's with_trashed flag (binding.rs:656, `_trashed`), which no test pins; binders are tested on plain Router routes only, never on group!, fluent group or resource! routes, nor on an Option<T> or RouteParam<T> argument, where adopt goes through RouteParam::__from_bound (framework/src/database/route_binding.rs:481-483). A binder whose resolver finds nothing now reaches a route's missing() handler under test (bind_009_a_binder_that_finds_nothing_reaches_the_missing_handler, missing.rs:184, BIND-009). A custom_route_binding model keeps the default route_binding_info unless it overrides it, so it records no columns (binding fields pass unchecked) and no children (a scoped child under it is refused); no test covers either for a #[model] (startup.rs:166 covers a non-model type without columns). manual/routing.md "Custom resolution" shows `#[model(table = "tags", custom_route_binding)]` and is not compiled.
requirement BIND-008 Q3 observed: (1) A soft-deleted row binding on a route without with_trashed(), or failing on one with it: RouteBuilder::with_trashed (framework/src/routing/router.rs:2571) sets RouteBindingOptions::with_trashed, the plan carries it (framework/src/routing/binding.rs:1138) and resolve_erased picks the soft-deletable lookup (binding.rs:242-255), which the generated lookups implement by lifting only the soft-delete filter (framework/src/database/route_binding.rs:719-722); bind_008_a_soft_deleted_row_binds_only_on_a_route_with_trashed (framework/tests/routing/route_binding/trashed.rs:118) asserts 404 and 200. As a parent: bind_008_a_soft_deleted_parent_binds_only_on_a_route_with_trashed (trashed.rs:187) declares a soft-deleting TrShelf with a books relation and asserts a trashed shelf answers 404 under a scoped child (`{book:slug}`) and an unscoped one, and 200 on the with_trashed() copies of both routes, while a live shelf binds on all; the parent goes through the same resolve call (binding.rs:1293). This closes the draft gap. (2) As a scoped child: child_erased picks resolve_soft_deletable_child_route_binding (binding.rs:257-281) and __route_child_lookup lifts the filter (route_binding.rs:814-817); bind_008_a_scoped_child_follows_with_trashed (trashed.rs:149). (3) Through the bare x::Model form: bare_lookup returns the row only when the table's #[model] soft-delete column is null, unless trashed (route_binding.rs:599-636, soft_delete_column_of at :503-507 over the SoftDeleteTable inventory emitted by suprnova-macros/src/model/route_binding.rs:147-152); bind_008_the_bare_form_follows_with_trashed (trashed.rs:135) and bind_003_the_bare_form_still_binds_by_primary_key (regressions.rs:78); bind_003_the_bare_form_binds_an_entity_no_model_defines (regressions.rs:168) asserts an entity no #[model] covers has no soft-deleted rows (a set deleted_at binds on both routes). (4) A model that replaces its binding receiving the plain lookup: resolve_erased dispatches on the route's flag for any RouteBinding type; bind_008_a_replaced_binding_receives_the_soft_deletable_lookup (trashed.rs:235) uses TrProbe, a hand-written non-model type that answers "plain" or "soft-deletable", and bind_008_a_model_binder_gives_a_replaced_binding_the_lookup_the_route_selects (trashed.rs:316) covers Router::model; bind_008_a_model_binder_follows_with_trashed (trashed.rs:291) covers a model() binder over a #[model]. No #[model(custom_route_binding)] model is used, though the dispatch is the same. (5) A resource's with_trashed() naming no actions missing show, edit or update, or reaching another action: the resource builders' with_trashed (framework/src/routing/resource.rs:456) and register_resource, which maps an empty list to Show, Edit and Update and sets the option per action (resource.rs:826-840, applied per route at :851); bind_008_a_resource_with_trashed_applies_to_show_edit_and_update (trashed.rs:250) asserts show, edit, PUT and PATCH update bind the trashed row, destroy stays 404, and a list naming Destroy reaches destroy alone. The global-scope part is bind_002_with_trashed_lifts_only_the_soft_delete_filter (trashed.rs:356). Not driven: with_trashed() set through get!(...) (framework/src/routing/macros.rs:219) or a MultiMethodRouteBuilder (router.rs:2989), separate setters of the same flag. All named tests are selected by `^route_binding::`; none is ignored or gated.
requirement BIND-008 Q4 observed: No code for this requirement changed since 6ee2773b2. Paths the change added that no test drives: with_trashed() on the route-macro builder (get!(...).with_trashed(), framework/src/routing/macros.rs:219-222) and on MultiMethodRouteBuilder (router.rs:2989); RouteBindingOptions::within ORs a group's with_trashed into its routes (framework/src/routing/binding.rs:566-577) though no group builder exposes with_trashed; Binder::bind ignores the flag (binding.rs:656); two #[model]s on one table, where the first SoftDeleteTable entry with a column wins (framework/src/database/route_binding.rs:503-507); the HasManyThrough intermediate soft-delete guard and the BelongsToMany child lookup under with_trashed (lift_soft_deletes applies to the child query only, route_binding.rs:814-817). Builder::lift_soft_deletes (framework/src/eloquent/builder.rs:2788) is new and runs only on SQLite in this mechanism; no Postgres or MySQL route uses soft deletes. manual/routing.md and manual/controllers.md describe with_trashed() and are not compiled. The statement above that no test drives with_trashed() on the route-macro builder or MultiMethodRouteBuilder no longer holds: since 37741ac5a, trashed.rs bind_008_every_with_trashed_setter_binds_a_trashed_row drives get!, any!, Router::any and Router::methods (macros.rs:220, router.rs:2994), each binding a trashed row and the same route without with_trashed() answering 404.
requirement BIND-009 Q3 observed: Enforced in framework/src/routing/binding.rs: bind_request (1232-1257) calls miss() when a non-optional parameter is absent (1245) or a lookup returns Ok(None) (1253), and miss() (1218-1227) returns the route's missing() response instead of FrameworkError::model_not_found unless the binding is a unit enum (1219-1220). A value that does not parse reaches miss() because resolve_model_route_binding returns Ok(None) when column.parse rejects it (framework/src/database/route_binding.rs:716-718), bare_lookup does the same for the primary key (route_binding.rs:619-621), and __route_child_lookup returns Ok(None) for an unparseable or unowned child (route_binding.rs:811-822); an explicit bind() binder that finds nothing takes the same path through lookup's binder arm (binding.rs:1269-1273). The hook reaches the plan as RoutePlan.missing (binding.rs:1139), set by RouteBuilder::missing (framework/src/routing/router.rs:2604), MultiMethodRouteBuilder::missing (router.rs:3023, behind Router::any and Router::methods), GroupBuilder::missing (framework/src/routing/group.rs:146, merged into each route at group.rs:256 through RouteBindingOptions::within, binding.rs:566-575), the route macro's missing (framework/src/routing/macros.rs:240), group!'s missing (macros.rs:1229, merged at 1387, 1474, 1527 and handed to a nested group at 1539) and the resource builders' missing (framework/src/routing/resource.rs:464, applied per action at resource.rs:838). Tests in framework/tests/routing/route_binding/missing.rs (module route_binding::missing, reachable from framework/tests/routing/main.rs:11 and route_binding/mod.rs, full names route_binding::missing::bind_009_* matched by test(/^route_binding::/), none ignored, no env guard, SQLite in memory); each asserts the hook's exact answer (302 and the body 'missing at <path>'), so a plain 404 fails it: bind_009_a_route_missing_handler_replaces_the_404 (missing.rs:78) for a missing row, a malformed value and an unowned scoped child on Router::get routes; bind_009_a_group_missing_handler_reaches_its_routes (missing.rs:106) through group! for a missing row, a malformed child (/g/posts/abc), a malformed parent (/g/users/abc/posts/ada-post) and an unowned child; bind_009_a_resource_missing_handler_reaches_its_routes (missing.rs:141) through resource! for a missing and a malformed row and, on a nested resource with scoped([("post", "slug")]), for a missing child, a missing parent, a malformed parent and an unowned child; bind_009_a_binder_that_finds_nothing_reaches_the_missing_handler (missing.rs:185) for a bind() binder that returns Ok(None), with a route without missing() keeping the 404; bind_009_every_missing_site_replaces_the_404 (missing.rs:213) for a missing and a malformed row through Router::get(..).missing, Router::any(..).missing, get!(..).missing, group!(..).missing, Router::group(..).missing and resource!(..).missing; bind_009_a_route_without_missing_answers_404 (missing.rs:280) is the control. These close the draft's four gaps: a malformed value under a group's missing(), a malformed or unowned binding under a resource's missing(), a bind() binder that finds no row, and the replaced 404 of the Router::group, get! and Router::any sites. Still without a test: a route inside a group! nested in a group that has missing(), which gets the hook only through the inheritance at macros.rs:1387 and 1539; every missing() test installs the hook on the innermost group, resource or route.
requirement BIND-009 Q4 observed: Also changed and not covered by a test the mechanism runs: the precedence in RouteBindingOptions::within (framework/src/routing/binding.rs:566-575), which makes a route's own missing() win over its group's, with no test of a route and its group both setting one; the inheritance into a nested group! (framework/src/routing/macros.rs:1387, 1539), listed as a remaining gap; Router::methods(..).missing, which shares MultiMethodRouteBuilder::missing (framework/src/routing/router.rs:3023) with Router::any and is driven by no test; the shared resource_spec_methods! missing() (framework/src/routing/resource.rs:464), which also compiles on Router::resource(...) for a ResourceController, whose actions carry no HandlerRecord (ResourceHandlers::handler, resource.rs:360-362), so build_plans makes no plan for those routes (binding.rs:912-914): the hook is checked at startup (the 'resource controller missing' site of missing_sites, framework/tests/routing/route_binding/startup.rs:650) but can never answer, since a controller action binds nothing; and bind_request's error arm (binding.rs:1247-1250), where a lookup that returns Err, such as a database error or a binder's own error, answers that error and never reaches missing(). The documentation, manual/routing.md '### Missing rows' (line 399) and '## Resource routing' (line 759), is checked by no test beyond the needles of route_binding::manual. The coverage commit c55dc5f9e added tests only and changed no framework code. The nested-group case listed above as a remaining gap is closed since 37741ac5a: missing.rs bind_009_an_outer_group_missing_handler_reaches_a_nested_groups_routes nests two groups in an outer group with missing() and expects the hook's response for a missing row, malformed parent and child, and unowned children; dropping the inheritance at macros.rs:1387 or :1539 fails it.
requirement BIND-010 Q3 observed: Enforced in suprnova-macros/src/route_binding_derive.rs: each variant's string is its #[route(value = "...")] or else snake_case of its name (line 39, snake_case at 126-135); resolve_route_binding matches the raw value against those literals exactly, every other value falling to None (54-56, 82-85); route_binding_info marks the type unit_enum (88-90). framework/src/routing/binding.rs miss() (1218-1227) skips the route's missing() when binding.info.is_unit_enum() (1220) and answers FrameworkError::model_not_found(info.name()) (framework/src/error.rs:1464-1469), where the name is short_type_name, the enum's bare type name (framework/src/database/route_binding.rs:241-249, 307-311), never the value. Tests in framework/tests/routing/route_binding/enums.rs (module route_binding::enums, selected by test(/^route_binding::/), not ignored, no database or env guard), on a route that installs a missing() handler answering 302 'missing ran', so a run of it fails each: bind_010_a_variant_binds_from_its_value_or_snake_case_name (enums.rs:36) asserts fruits, pantry_staples and veg bind Fruits, PantryStaples and Vegetables and that vegetables answers 404; bind_010_matching_is_exact_and_case_sensitive (enums.rs:58) asserts 404 for Fruits, FRUITS, fruit and pantry-staples; bind_010_a_miss_answers_404_without_missing_and_never_repeats_the_value (enums.rs:70) asserts status 404, message 'EnCategory not found' and a body without 'sweets'. Every falsifier clause has a test. The build test bind_010_an_enum_with_a_field_cannot_derive_route_binding (suprnova-macros/tests/route_binding_build.rs:289, fixture enum-fields; that binary runs unfiltered and has no ignored test) also runs, for the unit-only rule. Neither the enforcing code nor enums.rs changed since 6ee2773b2, and the draft listed no gap here.
requirement BIND-010 Q4 observed: The derive's other compile-time refusals in suprnova-macros/src/route_binding_derive.rs, a non-enum (16-22), generic parameters (23-28), two variants sharing one value (40-49), an empty value (111-113) and an unknown key inside #[route(...)] (117), are not reached by the mechanism: their unit tests route_binding_derive::tests::bind_010_variants_bind_from_their_value_or_snake_case_name, bind_010_a_variant_with_fields_is_refused, bind_010_two_variants_with_one_value_are_refused and bind_010_a_struct_is_refused (route_binding_derive.rs:149-179) fall outside the lib filter test(/^handler::tests::/), and only the fields case has a build fixture. snake_case is exercised only on PantryStaples, not on names with acronyms or digits. Also untested: RouteParam over an enum (RouteParam::route_binding_info delegates at framework/src/database/route_binding.rs:477-479, so the unit_enum flag carries; the RouteParam comparison in contract.rs covers CtEssay only), an enum parameter bound through a bind() binder, and the derive's registration with attributes(route) at suprnova-macros/src/lib.rs:204 beyond its use in enums.rs. The derived RouteValue (route_binding_derive.rs:93-97) is covered by bind_010_route_fills_a_parameter_with_the_variant_string (enums.rs:79), which passes the variant by value. The prose in manual/routing.md '### Enums' (line 411) and the feature-map vocabulary for #[route(value)] (commit 5bf54ce69) are checked only by the 'snake case' needle of bind_014_the_routing_chapter_says_why_suprnova_diverges.
requirement BIND-011 Q3 observed: Enforced in suprnova-macros/src/resource.rs expand (84-150), which applies only/except (114-133) and emits .__action(variant, module::function) for every selected action (146), so a selected action without its function fails to compile; and in framework/src/routing/resource.rs: ResourceDef::__action (677-688) keeps each function's HandlerRecord, so its bound arguments are planned like any handler's; ResourceLayout::of (723-761) lays out users.posts as /users/{user}/posts and /users/{user}/posts/{post} named users.posts.*, with parameters() renames (parameter_of, 763-773) and scoped() fields written as {post:slug}; register_resource (820-875) registers each action (PATCH beside PUT), notes its record and sets per-action RouteBindingOptions (826-839): scoped Some(true) once scoped() is called, with_trashed for the listed actions or show, edit and update, and missing; the ResourceController form still dispatches through make_handler (881-899). Tests in framework/tests/routing/route_binding/resources.rs (module route_binding::resources, selected by test(/^route_binding::/), not ignored, SQLite in memory): bind_011_a_function_resource_action_receives_a_bound_model (158), bind_011_api_resource_and_only_select_actions (187), bind_011_nested_resources_register_under_dotted_names (211; route('users.posts.show') gives /users/1/posts/1), bind_011_parameters_renames_the_parameters (234; the handlers read author and article, so an unrenamed path would be refused at startup), bind_011_scoped_gives_a_field_and_scopes_the_child (248; another user's post answers 404, scoped([]) still scopes) and bind_011_a_resource_controller_routes_as_it_did (272); the build test bind_011_a_selected_action_without_its_function_fails_the_build (suprnova-macros/tests/route_binding_build.rs:313, fixtures resource-missing, whose stderr must name destroy, and resource-selected, which must compile); bind_008_a_resource_with_trashed_applies_to_show_edit_and_update (trashed.rs:250) for with_trashed(); bind_009_a_resource_missing_handler_reaches_its_routes (missing.rs:141) for missing(), now also on a nested scoped resource. The draft's gap, that an existing ResourceController implementation routing differently was checked only for index, show and one nested match, is closed: bind_011_a_resource_controller_routes_as_it_did now registers the seven-action FullCtl controller under rsfull/posts and asserts each action under the verb and path the c04577b96 layout gave it (GET index, GET create, POST store, GET show, GET edit, PUT and PATCH update, DELETE destroy, each answering its own method's name and the post parameter), route() for all seven names rsfull.posts.index to rsfull.posts.destroy, that except([Destroy, Update]) keeps POST and edit routed and leaves PUT, PATCH and DELETE unrouted, and that a dotted controller name registers the nested path; a controller that dispatched an action to another method, dropped PATCH or renamed a route fails one of those assertions.
requirement BIND-011 Q4 observed: The rewrite of framework/src/routing/resource.rs moved the ResourceController form onto the shared ResourceLayout, so a controller registered under a dotted name such as users.posts now registers nested paths (/users/{user}/posts/{post}), where at c04577b96 try_register used the whole name as one literal segment (/users.posts/{users.post}, through resource_route and default_param_name); bind_011_a_resource_controller_routes_as_it_did asserts the new nested layout (the rsusers.posts match), so this change of route for an existing dotted-name controller is tested as intended rather than guarded against, and is listed for the developer. Not reached by the mechanism, which runs only --test routing of the framework: the in-source tests resource::tests (framework/src/routing/resource.rs:1010, web_resource_registers_seven_routes, update_action_registers_both_put_and_patch, parameter_overrides_path_parameter_name and the rest); authorize_resource on either form (resource.rs:531) and framework/tests/resources/resource_authorization.rs, another binary; names(), rename() and parameter() on resource! (resource.rs:389, 404, 415), ResourceDef::try_register (701), Router::resources and api_resources (984, 997); the store action of a function-form resource, registered but requested by no test (show, edit, PUT, PATCH and DELETE are requested in resources.rs and trashed.rs:250); the macro's refusals of an unknown key (suprnova-macros/src/resource.rs:62-68) and an unknown action (95-112), whose unit tests resource::tests::bind_011_a_resource_names_every_action_function, bind_011_only_and_except_name_just_the_selected_functions and bind_011_an_unknown_action_is_refused (166-195) are outside the handler::tests:: filter; and scoped() and with_trashed() on a ResourceController, which compile through resource_spec_methods! but have no plan to act on because controller actions carry no record. The TypeScript route generator (suprnova-cli/src/commands/generate_routes.rs collect_routes, 158-172) makes routes only from get!, post!, put!, patch!, delete! and group!, so resource! and api_resource! routes get no TypeScript helpers, which nothing checks. manual/routing.md '## Resource routing' (759-927) and manual/controllers.md are unchecked. Router::resource("users.posts", Ctl) registered /users.posts/{users.post} at c04577b96 and now registers /users/{user}/posts/{post}; the integrator read BIND-011's nested-resource clause as governing a dotted name, so an application that registered a ResourceController under a dotted name gets nested routes.
requirement BIND-012 Q3 observed: Enforced in framework/src/routing/route_values.rs: RouteValue (31-36) and bound_route_value (40-45), which fills a {post:slug} parameter with route_field(field) and any other with route_key; RouteParameters for one positional value filling the route's first parameter (240-244), for one named pair (247-253), for tuples of two to six named pairs (255-287), and for the string-pair containers a &[(&str, &str)] parameter took (string_pair_containers at 131-137, the borrowed and mutable arrays at 139-149, the per-length DerefText arrays at 190-237). #[model] emits RouteValue through bound_route_value (suprnova-macros/src/model/route_binding.rs:141-145), as do the enum derive (route_binding_derive.rs:93-97) and RouteParam (route_values.rs:65-69). framework/src/routing/router.rs route (551-559) and try_route (623-637) read the registered pattern, which keeps its binding fields, and hand each placeholder's name and field to the parameters through placeholder_value (381-387), encoding the value with encode_placeholder (391-401). Tests in framework/tests/routing/route_binding/urls.rs (module route_binding::urls, selected by test(/^route_binding::/), not ignored, SQLite in memory): bind_012_a_bound_value_fills_its_route_key_or_its_binding_field (urls.rs:124; the id for &post on {post}, the percent-encoded slug a%20b%2Fc on {post:slug}, the same through RouteParam(post) by value, the route_key column for UrPage); bind_012_named_values_mix_strings_and_bound_values (159); bind_012_try_route_takes_bound_values_and_reports_missing_ones (175); bind_012_a_hand_written_binding_and_a_bare_model_are_bound_values_through_route_value (249; a hand-written RouteBinding type and ur_post::Model, positional and named, through route and try_route; refusing a RouteValue type would stop the routing binary compiling); bind_012_calls_written_with_strings_produce_the_same_urls (192) and bind_012_string_pairs_in_every_container_written_today_fill_as_before (216) for unchanged string URLs. The build test bind_012_every_string_pair_form_written_before_route_binding_compiles (suprnova-macros/tests/route_binding_build.rs:324, fixture url-strings; the binary runs unfiltered, none ignored) checks 27 route and 5 try_route string forms plus the four rewritten ones. A #[model] row passed by value, literally route("posts.show", post), is not exercised; &post and RouteParam(post) go through the same generic impl, and an enum variant by value is (enums.rs:79). The draft listed no gap here; neither the enforcing code nor urls.rs changed since 6ee2773b2.
requirement BIND-012 Q4 observed: Not covered: the string-only URL builders the change left on the old shapes, route_with_params and try_route_with_params (framework/src/routing/router.rs:565, 642), which the Redirect::route builder uses (framework/src/http/response.rs:1509-1513), and url::signed_route and temporary_signed_route (framework/src/routing/url.rs:182, 196) and Redirect::signed_route and temporary_signed_route (response.rs:957, 964), which keep &[(&str, &str)]; no bound value reaches them and their handling of a {post:slug} pattern is not tested here. The numeric RouteValue impls (route_values.rs:71-85) and a positional string such as route('x', '7') are new and covered only by route_values::tests (one_value_fills_the_first_parameter_only, route_values.rs:306, and others), which the mechanism does not run. The lenient substitute writes a missing optional {post:slug?} back with its field (router.rs:353-357), untested. route() callers outside the framework crate, app/src/controllers/user.rs:105 among them, are not compiled by the mechanism. route() and try_route() also gained the public root (router.rs:558, 631, PFX-003), which these tests see only as an empty root. manual/urls.md '### Bound values' (lines 84-140), which carries the bound_route_value example and the four rewritten forms the requirement says the manual shows, and the CHANGELOG entry are unchecked.
requirement BIND-013 Q3 observed: Enforced in framework/src/routing/binding.rs check_handler (950-1000): for every HandlerArg of kind Bound or PathValue whose name no placeholder of the route's path declares (978-984) it records 'route `METHOD pattern`: handler `path` reads the route parameter `name` (`name: Type`), which the route's path does not declare'; build_plans (876-942) runs it for every recorded route, for the fallback against an empty path (879-889) and for a recorded missing() hook (902-911), and returns the joined problems as a FrameworkError, which Server::from_config (framework/src/server.rs:150), Server::finish_boot (server.rs:219, behind try_from_config_with_routes and the async constructor Application serves through, framework/src/app/mod.rs:106) and Server::run (server.rs:382) return, and handle_request answers (server.rs:1062-1064). The record comes from #[handler] (suprnova-macros/src/handler.rs record_items, 589-651): a primitive is HandlerArg::path_value, and a probed type that implements RouteBinding records as Bound through __ArgProbe's __record (binding.rs:450-454, and 490-494 for Option); RouteParam<T> and the bare x::Model form are probed types that implement RouteBinding, so they meet the same arm. Tests in framework/tests/routing/route_binding/startup.rs and associated.rs (modules route_binding::startup and route_binding::associated, selected by test(/^route_binding::/), none ignored): bind_013_a_bound_argument_for_an_undeclared_parameter_is_refused (startup.rs:179; #[model] SuPost on /users/{user}; asserts 'GET /users/{user}', `post` and 'does not declare'); bind_013_a_route_param_or_bare_argument_for_an_undeclared_parameter_is_refused (startup.rs:200), which closes the draft's gap: handlers taking RouteParam<SuPost> and su_post::Model on /users/{user} must each be refused naming the route, `post`, the argument's type and 'does not declare', and both must start on /wrapped/{post} and /bare/{post}; bind_013_a_primitive_for_an_undeclared_parameter_is_refused (startup.rs:229; id: i64 on /users/{user}; asserts the route and `id`); bind_004_every_registration_site_is_checked (startup.rs:256; 25 registration sites, among them the fluent group's eight verbs, methods and try_methods and a nested group!, each refused naming `id`); bind_013_a_fallback_reading_an_undeclared_parameter_is_refused_through_handle_request (startup.rs:576); bind_013_a_missing_handler_reading_an_undeclared_parameter_is_refused (startup.rs:661; seven installation sites, each naming '`missing()` handler', `id` and /posts/{post}); bind_013_a_refused_missing_handler_refuses_a_router_driven_through_handle_request (startup.rs:688); bind_003_an_associated_handler_reading_an_undeclared_parameter_is_refused (associated.rs:134). That a refused route does not start is asserted by bind_004_a_refusal_is_an_error_from_the_boot_path (startup.rs:391; Server::from_config, try_from_config_with_routes, try_from_config_with_routes_async and Server::run on port 0 each return the refusal naming the route and `post`, and a panic or a boot that succeeds fails the test) and bind_004_a_refusal_stops_the_application_boot_without_a_panic (startup.rs:483; an Application serving the refused router through run_with_args, in a child process of the test binary, must exit 1 with the refusal and no panic).
requirement BIND-013 Q4 observed: Beyond those tests: the last step from Application::run to the process exit is argued by construction, not run: Application::run (framework/src/app/mod.rs:1083) calls run_cli_or_exit (1165-1169), which exits 1 when run_cli returns an error, and bind_004_a_refusal_stops_the_application_boot_without_a_panic proves the refusal reaches the caller of run_with_args (mod.rs:1137), whose child test exits 1 itself. The check also refuses an undeclared optional path value (Option<i64>, recorded with optional true by handler.rs record_items), which no test covers. The dogfood app (app/src/routes.rs and app/src/controllers) and the scaffold routes (suprnova-cli/src/templates/files/backend/routes.rs.tpl, suprnova-cli/src/templates/files/api/src/routes.rs.tpl) now boot through this check; the mechanism neither builds nor starts them (app is not in its package list, and scaffold_snapshot is #[ignore]d at suprnova-cli/tests/scaffold_snapshot.rs:389 and 399 and outside the mechanism). manual/routing.md '### Startup checks' (line 432) is checked only by the 'at startup' needle of bind_014_the_routing_chapter_says_why_suprnova_diverges.
requirement BIND-014 Q3 observed: Enforced by the documentation itself: manual/routing.md '## Route model binding' (line 234) with its '### Older binding forms' (460) and '### Why Suprnova diverges' (505), manual/tutorial-inertia-crud.md '## 3. Model' (84), '## 4. Controller' (122) and '## 5. Routes' (240), and the removal of the old trait and macro from framework/src/database/route_binding.rs. Tests in framework/tests/routing/route_binding/manual.rs (module route_binding::manual, selected by test(/^route_binding::/), not ignored, no env guard): bind_014_no_page_or_doc_comment_names_the_removed_surface (manual.rs:178) scans every top-level manual/*.md page and the rustdoc of every .rs file under src/ of every workspace member read from the root Cargo.toml (it asserts suprnova-cli/src/main.rs is among them) for route_binding!, 'route_binding macro' or param_name with or without () (names_removed_surface, manual.rs:167-175, which skips a line naming ParamError, whose field is called param_name); its reader rustdoc_text (manual.rs:89-141) takes ///, //!, /** */ and /*! */ blocks and the string literals of #[doc = ...] and #![doc = ...] attributes. bind_014_no_page_teaches_the_bare_form_as_the_way_to_bind_a_model (manual.rs:267) flags every fn signature, on one line or several and holding parentheses, whose parameters name a path ending in x::Model (bare_form_signatures, manual.rs:208-244), on every manual page, on routing.md with only its '### Older binding forms' section blanked (the test asserts that section exists and alone is left out), and in the rustdoc of every workspace crate. bind_014_the_scans_read_what_they_claim (manual.rs:307) proves on fixed text that the readers see each doc-comment form, a mention without (), a multi-line signature with parentheses and a longer path, and that blanking keeps line numbers. bind_014_the_routing_chapter_says_why_suprnova_diverges (manual.rs:345) requires the binding section's '### Why Suprnova diverges' to contain 'does not parse', 'never repeats the value', 'snake case' and 'at startup'. suprnova-macros/tests/route_binding_build.rs bind_014_the_tutorial_model_controller_and_routes_compile (route_binding_build.rs:333; the binary runs unfiltered, none ignored) writes the first rust block after each of the three headings and the three Svelte pages into the tutorial fixture member and runs a real cargo check of route-binding-tutorial, so the mechanism builds the tutorial from the manual. These close both draft gaps: the first scan now reads mentions without (), #[doc] attributes, doc blocks and every workspace crate; the second now reads rustdoc, all of routing.md but its older-forms section, multi-line signatures and any path ending in ::Model. Limits that are not falsifier clauses: both scans read src/ only, so rustdoc under a crate's tests/ or examples/ and the excluded crates/suprnova-live/fuzz is not read, and the bare-form scan sees fn signatures only, not prose or a closure.
requirement BIND-014 Q4 observed: The change rewrote binding prose in manual/routing.md, manual/controllers.md, manual/authorization.md, manual/urls.md, manual/from-laravel.md, manual/from-rust-web.md, manual/macros.md, manual/glossary.md, manual/http-tests.md and manual/eloquent-relationships.md; apart from the tutorial's three blocks and the scans above, none of their Rust examples is compiled by the mechanism. The rustdoc examples in framework/src/database/route_binding.rs (6-15, 65-73), framework/src/routing/route_values.rs (8-13, 24-30) and framework/src/routing/resource.rs (516-526, 642-649) are rust,ignore and never compiled; resource.rs's rust,no_run blocks (35-43, 937-957) compile only as doctests, which the mechanism does not run. The tutorial check is a cargo check of the fixture crate (suprnova with default features off): it does not build the tutorial's routes into a Router or call prepare_bindings, so the startup check the tutorial describes after its routes block (manual/tutorial-inertia-crud.md:260-265) is not exercised on those routes. The CHANGELOG.md entry for route binding is unchecked. The scans in manual.rs parse the root Cargo.toml's member list by splitting on quotes (manual.rs:51-69) and assert only that more than four members were read.

A form declared first answering 422 for a missing row: the plan binds before the handler (binding.rs planned_handler 1198-1213 and bind_request 1232-1257, wired at server.rs:1072-1073) and the macro emits the bind pass before the path and body passes (handler.rs:399-402); bind_015_a_missing_row_answers_404_before_the_body_is_validated (handler_form.rs:158), handler::tests::bind_015_bound_arguments_come_before_the_body_without_authorize (1275) and authorize_param_form_checks_after_binding_and_before_body_and_form (1396). Two body readers starting: check_handler (binding.rs:964-974); bind_015_two_body_readers_are_refused_at_startup (handler_form.rs:202), startup.rs bind_015_a_missing_handler_with_two_body_readers_is_refused (675), handler::tests::bind_015_two_body_readers_are_left_to_the_startup_check (1045). Option<T> answering 400: __OptionalArgProbe (binding.rs:490-532), optional_bound (358-386) and bind_request skipping an absent optional binding (1242-1244); bind_015_an_option_binds_none_when_its_optional_parameter_is_absent (handler_form.rs:179), startup.rs bind_004_an_optional_binding_field_registers_an_optional_parameter (129), handler::tests::bind_015_an_option_binds_none_when_its_parameter_is_absent (930), and keys.rs bind_002_a_malformed_value_answers_404_for_a_scoped_child_and_an_optional_argument (197), added in c55dc5f9e, which asserts /maybe answers 'none' and a malformed value answers the missing-row 404. The TypeScript generator: suprnova-cli/src/commands/generate_routes.rs placeholder and without_binding_field (385-414) read {post:slug} as post, and scan_routes takes the form request only from arguments not named after a path parameter (765-775); route_binding_build.rs bind_015_the_typescript_route_generator_reads_binding_fields_and_bound_arguments (371) runs the ts-routes fixture binary over suprnova_cli::commands::generate_routes::{scan_routes, generate_typescript} and asserts 'route posts.update params post request UpdatePost' and no 'post:slug' in the TypeScript, so the generator is exercised although suprnova-cli is not in the mechanism's package list. requirement BIND-015 Q4 observed: The #[handler] rewrite (suprnova-macros/src/handler.rs) changes the expansion of every handler in the workspace, while the mechanism compiles only the suprnova crate with its routing tests, suprnova-macros and the route-binding fixtures: handlers in app/, crates/suprnova-live, crates/suprnova-magnetar, the payments crates and the scaffold templates (suprnova-cli/src/templates/files/backend/controllers/*.rs.tpl, suprnova-cli/src/templates/files/api/src/controllers/users.rs.tpl) are not compiled by it. The generator change in suprnova-cli/src/commands/generate_routes.rs visits only free functions (visit_item_fn, 525-556), so a #[handler(Self = Type)] function inside an impl block, which BIND-003 added, gets no form-request typing, and the generator's own unit tests in that file (form_request_scan_tests at 1234, route_contract_tests at 1295) are not run by the mechanism, which reaches the generator only through the ts-routes fixture's single route. The fallback in HandlerInput::optional_bound (framework/src/routing/binding.rs:376-385), through which a handler without a plan resolves a bound argument by its route key without missing(), with_trashed() or scoping, is reached by no test (bind_015_a_generic_argument_reads_the_body_even_when_its_type_binds, handler_form.rs:236, takes the body path). An Option of a form request, read through __OptionalArgProbe's FromRequest impl (binding.rs:512-532), is covered only by expansion tests, not through a router. manual/controllers.md, manual/routing.md and manual/macros.md are unchecked beyond the BIND-014 scans.
requirement PFX-001 Q3 observed: Enforcement (unchanged since 6ee2773b2): framework/src/server.rs:752-765 request_root asks AppConfig.trusted_proxies.trusts(peer_ip) and hands the answer to framework/src/routing/root.rs:67-76 resolve, which reads the header only when the peer is trusted; root.rs:92-103 forwarded_prefix returns None when a second header line exists (root.rs:95), when the value is not visible ASCII (to_str, root.rs:98), maps exactly `/` to the empty root (root.rs:99-101), and otherwise requires valid_prefix (root.rs:106-115: ASCII letters, digits, `-` `.` `_` `~` `/`, a leading `/`, no empty, `.` or `..` segment, which also refuses a trailing `/`, `billing` and the empty value); server.rs:741-743 installs the root once per request. Clauses and tests, all in framework/tests/path_prefix/trust.rs: untrusted peer, pfx_001_a_prefix_from_an_untrusted_peer_reaches_no_url (trust.rs:54); rule-breaking values (`%2e%2e`, `/%2e%2e`, `/caf` plus the UTF-8 bytes of U+00E9, `/a/../b`, `/a/./b`, `/a//b`, `/billing/`, `billing`, `/a,/b`), pfx_001_a_value_that_breaks_the_rule_reaches_no_url (trust.rs:73); two header lines, pfx_001_a_prefix_sent_on_two_lines_is_ignored_whole (trust.rs:114); a valid prefix from a trusted proxy, pfx_001_a_valid_prefix_from_a_trusted_proxy_becomes_the_root (trust.rs:35); a trusted `/` with APP_URL https://example.org/app, pfx_001_a_trusted_slash_makes_the_root_the_host_root (trust.rs:136); a trusted empty value, pfx_001_a_trusted_empty_value_is_ignored (trust.rs:155, expects `/app|/app/probe`). Each asserts both url::root() and route() for the probe route, the two places a prefix reaches a URL. The manual clause is asserted by pfx_001_the_manual_says_the_proxy_sets_or_clears_the_prefix_header (framework/tests/path_prefix/manual.rs:113, the string `sets or clears `X-Forwarded-Prefix`` inside the `## Serving under a path prefix` section of manual/deployment.md, line 511). Each framework/tests/path_prefix test named here carries pfx_0 in its name, so check-path-prefix.sh's `cargo nextest run -p suprnova --test path_prefix -E 'test(/pfx_0/)'` selects it (the binary is auto-discovered from framework/tests/path_prefix/main.rs, which declares every module cited); none is #[ignore]d or cfg-gated, which matters because the mechanism passes no --run-ignored; each one that drives a server re-runs its body in a child through own_process_async::delegate or own_process::run_alone, whose assert_child_passed (framework/tests/support/own_process.rs:72) fails unless the child ran exactly one test and passed it; the plain #[test] functions run directly. Limit: the rule-breaking and two-line cases run with APP_URL http://localhost, which has no path, so they show the value is ignored but not that the root then falls back to a non-empty APP_URL path; that fallback is asserted only for the empty value. The `/a b` value and the APP_URL guard are asserted only by the in-source tests in framework/src/routing/root.rs (mod tests, root.rs:250), which this mechanism does not run (it passes no --lib).
requirement PFX-001 Q4 observed: The change added framework/src/routing/root.rs (now 362 lines; since 6ee2773b2 it also gained prefixed_owned and rooted_owned, root.rs:218-237, pub(crate) helpers for MEM-003 that return the caller's String at the host root) and the per-request root in framework/src/server.rs:719-765 and framework/src/http/request.rs (public_root field, with_public_root and public_root at request.rs:523-541). Since 8f29328e1, root.rs pfx_010_url_to_and_secure_put_a_relative_path_under_the_origin_and_root calls url::to("x") and url::secure("x") inside a request behind a trusted prefix (http://example.org/billing/x and https://example.org/billing/x, for an APP_URL with no path and with /other) and at the host root (http://example.org/x); building the relative branch from APP_URL instead of the request root (framework/src/routing/url.rs:81) fails it.
requirement PFX-010 Q4 observed: Because the rule sits in into_hyper, it governs navigation headers an application writes itself with HttpResponse::header("Location", ...) (the `raw` case, redirects.rs:45) as well as the framework's own. The amendment of 2026-10-06 keeps url::to resolving a relative path against the origin and root, as it did from APP_URL before; url::to("?q") and url::to("#f") go through the same join and gain a `/` before them (join_base_path, url.rs:397-406, gives https://example.org/billing/?q), behaviour the amended text does not name for url::to and no test asserts. Not checked: an application route whose own path begins with the deployment prefix (documented as unsupported in manual/deployment.md:566-572). No enforcing code for this requirement changed since 6ee2773b2; 11f164518 added only tests.
requirement PFX-011 Q3 observed: Existence and output: framework/src/routing/url.rs:160-162 pub fn root returns root::current() (framework/src/routing/root.rs:55-59) as a String with no trailing slash (split_app_url trims it, root.rs:153-165). pfx_011_url_root_returns_the_root_without_a_trailing_slash (framework/tests/path_prefix/root.rs:190) asserts /billing behind the prefix, the empty string at the host root and /shop for APP_URL https://example.org/shop/ inside a request; pfx_002_outside_a_request_the_root_is_the_app_url_path (root.rs:140) asserts /billing outside a request. Manual: pfx_011_every_component_asset_link_in_the_manual_writes_the_root (framework/tests/path_prefix/manual.rs:195) reads every manual/*.md chapter and, through links_in (manual.rs:165-192), every attribute value after a name and `=` (double-quoted, single-quoted or bare), every Markdown link target and link definition and every CSS url() and @import; each value holding `-ui/` that is not absolute or network-path must start with `{{ suprnova::url::root() }}/`. pfx_011_the_link_reader_sees_every_link_form (manual.rs:222) requires the reader to find each of those nine forms and none in prose or an askama import, so a reader that skipped a form fails; this closes the draft's limit that only double-quoted href and src were read. pfx_011_the_manual_names_url_root_for_links_route_does_not_build (manual.rs:270) checks manual/urls.md and manual/live.md. Header bar: crates/suprnova-live/components/header-bar/header-bar.html:10-12 writes `{{ suprnova::url::root() }}/` when brand_href is empty; pfx_011_the_header_bar_default_brand_link_carries_the_root (root.rs:217) renders the shipped view through askama (framework/tests/path_prefix/askama.toml points at crates/suprnova-live/components) and asserts href="/billing/" behind the prefix and href="/" at the host root. Each framework/tests/path_prefix test named here carries pfx_0 in its name, so check-path-prefix.sh's `cargo nextest run -p suprnova --test path_prefix -E 'test(/pfx_0/)'` selects it (the binary is auto-discovered from framework/tests/path_prefix/main.rs, which declares every module cited); none is #[ignore]d or cfg-gated, which matters because the mechanism passes no --run-ignored; each one that drives a server re-runs its body in a child through own_process_async::delegate or own_process::run_alone, whose assert_child_passed (framework/tests/support/own_process.rs:72) fails unless the child ran exactly one test and passed it; the plain #[test] functions run directly.

requirement PFX-012 Q3 observed: Page props: framework/src/inertia/root_share.rs:59-74 RootShare::share inserts `root` = url::root() after the inner provider's props (root_share.rs:69-72), so an inner `root` cannot replace it. Scaffold registration: suprnova-cli/src/templates/files/backend/bootstrap.rs.tpl:213 inside register_http_stack (bootstrap.rs.tpl:90), which cmd/main.rs.tpl:12 runs through `.http_bootstrap(|| async { bootstrap::register_http_stack() })`. pfx_012_root_share_gives_every_page_the_root (framework/tests/path_prefix/inertia.rs:390) now runs the scaffold's own statement (run_quoted!, defined at inertia.rs:378 and used at 402, executes App::register_inertia_shared(Arc::new(RootShare::around(Arc::new(LocaleShare)))) and returns its text), requires bootstrap.rs.tpl to hold exactly one uncommented register_inertia_shared line, equal to that statement apart from whitespace and inside register_http_stack, and main.rs.tpl to run register_http_stack through http_bootstrap; it then serves a page and asserts props.root /billing behind the prefix, the empty string at the host root, and the inner `lang` prop still present. pfx_012_the_scaffold_registers_the_root_share (suprnova-cli/tests/template_drift.rs:2288) asserts the template's substring. Together these narrow the draft's gap: a scaffold that registered another provider, a second one after it, or registered it outside the HTTP boot fails; what remains is that no selected test compiles or serves a scaffolded application (scaffold_snapshot, suprnova-cli/tests/scaffold_snapshot.rs:389 and 399, is #[ignore]d and belongs to another mechanism), so the clause rests on the template text plus the served statement. Literal paths: pfx_012_scaffold_pages_build_every_url_from_the_root_prop (template_drift.rs:2347) walks the 21 non-Home pages of the three frontends (asserting the count) and, since 11f164518, flags every non-comment line (is_comment, template_drift.rs:2311-2317) in which a quote, double quote or backquote is followed by `/` (literal_path, template_drift.rs:2301-2309, except `/>`), so a template-literal path, a delete, put or patch call, a Vue :href binding and a form action are refused along with post, visit, get and href; it still requires each page to read usePage<{ root: string }>().props and use `${root}/`. pfx_012_the_literal_path_reader_sees_every_form (template_drift.rs:2319) requires the reader to flag nine such forms and pass `${root}/...` forms, closing the draft's needle-list gap. The template_drift tests are plain #[test] functions selected by its `-E 'test(/pfx_0/)'` run and not ignored. Each framework/tests/path_prefix test named here carries pfx_0 in its name, so check-path-prefix.sh's `cargo nextest run -p suprnova --test path_prefix -E 'test(/pfx_0/)'` selects it (the binary is auto-discovered from framework/tests/path_prefix/main.rs, which declares every module cited); none is #[ignore]d or cfg-gated, which matters because the mechanism passes no --run-ignored; each one that drives a server re-runs its body in a child through own_process_async::delegate or own_process::run_alone, whose assert_child_passed (framework/tests/support/own_process.rs:72) fails unless the child ran exactly one test and passed it; the plain #[test] functions run directly.
