requirement PFX-013 Q3 observed: Enforcement (unchanged since 6ee2773b2): framework/src/http/cookie.rs:585-612 parse_cookies keeps the first literal of a name (the literal_names.insert guard at cookie.rs:600-604) and never lets a later alias replace it (cookie.rs:605-609). Every cookie the framework reads goes through Request::cookies and Request::cookie (framework/src/http/request.rs:626-651, parse at 634), the only request Cookie-header parser in framework/src: the session cookie (framework/src/session/middleware.rs:1246), the remember-me cookie (middleware.rs:1648) and the maintenance bypass (has_valid_bypass_cookie, framework/src/app/maintenance.rs:596-597); the framework does not read the XSRF cookie server-side (framework/src/csrf/middleware.rs has no request cookie read). Tests in framework/tests/path_prefix/cookies.rs: pfx_013_parse_cookies_keeps_the_first_cookie_of_a_name (cookies.rs:261, a plain #[test] with the falsifier's exact header `suprnova_session=scoped; suprnova_session=stale`, plus a literal beating an earlier alias); pfx_013_the_session_middleware_loads_the_session_sent_first (cookies.rs:292; two real session cookies: the first sent loads, the reversed order loads the other); pfx_013_every_framework_cookie_reads_the_first_of_a_name (cookies.rs:274; XSRF-TOKEN, remember_me and suprnova_maintenance read through Request::cookie in a handler give `scoped`); and, since 11f164518, pfx_013_the_remember_me_and_bypass_readers_take_the_first_cookie (cookies.rs:319), which drives the readers themselves and closes the draft's gap: the remember-me reader leaves an encrypted carrier of an unknown version alone and forgets an undecryptable one with one clearing cookie (middleware.rs:1665-1670), so `later; garbage` must set no remember_me cookie and `garbage; later` exactly one; the bypass reader must answer 200 for `valid; garbage` and 503 for `garbage; valid`. A reader that took the second cookie fails each pair. Each framework/tests/path_prefix test named here carries pfx_0 in its name, so check-path-prefix.sh's `cargo nextest run -p suprnova --test path_prefix -E 'test(/pfx_0/)'` selects it (the binary is auto-discovered from framework/tests/path_prefix/main.rs, which declares every module cited); none is #[ignore]d or cfg-gated, which matters because the mechanism passes no --run-ignored; each one that drives a server re-runs its body in a child through own_process_async::delegate or own_process::run_alone, whose assert_child_passed (framework/tests/support/own_process.rs:72) fails unless the child ran exactly one test and passed it; the plain #[test] functions run directly.
requirement PFX-013 Q4 observed: The parse_cookies change (framework/src/http/cookie.rs:597-609) also altered the alias rule: of two percent-encoded aliases of one name the first now wins, where the last won before. The rule is stated in the rustdoc (cookie.rs:572-576) and manual/deployment.md:561-563; no test checks the manual sentence. The in-source cookie.rs unit tests (from cookie.rs:717) are not run by this mechanism. Besides request.rs:634, framework/src calls parse_cookies only in the testing helper framework/src/testing/response.rs:163, which reads a Set-Cookie pair, not a request; application code that relied on the last of two same-name cookies winning changes behaviour without a test here. Nothing under this requirement changed since 6ee2773b2; 11f164518 added only the reader test.
requirement IMG-001 Q3 observed: (1) Every orientation of a JPEG, PNG or WebP exact under either driver: the tag is read by metadata::source_orientation (framework/src/media/metadata.rs:879-887) through jpeg_exif (metadata.rs:313), the first eXIf via png_chunk (376) and webp_exif (606), parsed by Orientation::from_tiff with image::metadata::Orientation (framework/src/media/orientation.rs:24), and applied by Orientation::apply (orientation.rs:72) in OxideAvImageDriver::load (framework/src/media/oxideav.rs:302-304, 336-338), or under magick by the fixed transform Orienting::args (framework/src/media/magick.rs:393-411) built from that same tag (magick.rs:878-879, known at 892) and placed after the input by Run::args (magick.rs:452-479); img_001_every_orientation_is_applied_by_oxideav and img_001_every_orientation_is_applied_by_magick (framework/tests/media/images.rs:713, 720) run tags 1 to 8 through assert_every_orientation_is_applied (images.rs:685) on baseline JPEG, PNG, lossless and lossy WebP sources and, new since the draft, the colour and grey lossless JPEG fixtures (oriented_lossless_jpegs, images.rs:662; fixtures/jpeg/photo-lossless-rgb-35x21.jpg and photo-lossless-grey-35x21.jpg are SOF3 frames, the JpegLayout::Lossless path at oxideav.rs:399), to PNG, and compare with an independent permutation turned() (images.rs:611) of the driver's own opt-out decode, backed by the unit test img_001_every_orientation_is_the_exact_permutation_it_names (orientation.rs:133); this closes the draft's lossless-JPEG gap. (2) oxideav orients by the last of two Exif APP1 segments: jpeg_exif keeps the last segment before EOI; img_001_a_jpeg_with_two_exif_segments_is_oriented_by_the_last (images.rs:726), img_001_oxideav_reads_the_orientation_from_the_shared_sources (images.rs:958, through assert_one_reader_orients at images.rs:928, which adds a progressive JPEG with a tag between its scans) and the unit test img_001_a_tag_between_scans_is_the_last_one (metadata.rs:1659). (3) No inflation to read the tag: source_orientation reads only uncompressed bytes; img_001_reading_the_tag_inflates_no_compressed_chunk (framework/tests/memory/images.rs:972) puts a zTXt that inflates to 64 MiB before the eXIf and asserts a dhat peak under 4 MiB through OxideAvImageDriver::dimensions, which runs the same load and reader; the magick driver calls the same reader (magick.rs:879, 948) but no test measures it. (4) Oriented decode within the estimate its refusal names: peak::oriented (framework/src/media/oxideav/peak.rs:195) is applied in load (oxideav.rs:310-312); img_001_an_oriented_decode_stays_within_its_estimate (memory/images.rs:921) decodes oriented PNG, JPEG, lossless and lossy WebP through decoded_dimensions and asserts the peak is at most the named estimate and that one byte less is refused (tag 6 only). (5) orient() exists, applies the tag under the opt-out and never twice: Image::orient (framework/src/media/mod.rs:593), the Transformation::Orient arm guarded by Steps::applied (oxideav.rs:1094-1103) and the applied flag in Run::args (magick.rs:452-479); img_001_orient_applies_the_tag_once_under_oxideav and _under_magick (images.rs:791, 798), img_001_orient_after_a_custom_step_under_oxideav and _under_magick (images.rs:1799, 1806), and the unit test img_001_orientation_is_applied_after_the_input_or_at_its_step (magick.rs:2005). (6) The opt-out keeps the sensor's pixels: img_001_the_opt_out_keeps_the_sensor_pixels_under_oxideav and _under_magick (images.rs:819, 826), PNG source, and for a TIFF under magick the test in (9). (7) IMAGE_AUTO_ORIENT=false turns orientation on decode off: parse_auto_orient (mod.rs:218) in ImageConfig::from_env (mod.rs:299); img_001_image_auto_orient_false_turns_orientation_off (images.rs:833) starts its _child (images.rs:847) in a new process with the variable set and asserts ImageConfig::from_env, suprnova::media::config() and an oxideav decode, and the unit test img_001_image_auto_orient_reads_like_the_other_flags (mod.rs:1239) covers the accepted spellings; the _child returns at once unless SUPRNOVA_TEST_OWN_PROCESS is set, and the parent fails unless own_process::assert_child_passed sees exactly one passed test. (8) The clause the 2026-10-06 amendment adds, magick taking the orientation of a JPEG, PNG or WebP from anywhere but where oxideav reads it: for the five formats the framework reads, MagickCliDriver::process takes the tag from source_orientation (magick.rs:878-879) and Orienting::args emits only a fixed -rotate, -flip, -flop, -transpose or -transverse with +repage, never -auto-orient (magick.rs:393-411); img_001_magick_reads_the_orientation_from_the_shared_sources (images.rs:965) fails under ImageMagick's own reading, which the Rationale says takes the first Exif segment, PNG raw-profile text and orNT: it expects tag 6 from a JPEG whose two segments hold 3 then 6, 6 from the progressive JPEG with 3 in the header and 6 between scans (progressive_with_a_tag_between_scans, images.rs:892), and no turn from a PNG whose EXIF sits only in a zTXt Raw profile type exif chunk (raw_profile_exif, images.rs:910) or only in an orNT chunk; the unit test img_001_orientation_is_applied_after_the_input_or_at_its_step (magick.rs:2005-2066) asserts -auto-orient is absent for a JPEG and the fixed transform for each of the eight tags. No WebP case tells the two readings apart, and none is known: both read the WebP's first EXIF chunk (webp_chunk, metadata.rs:594-602). (9) -auto-orient only for a format the framework cannot read: img_001_magick_orients_a_format_the_framework_cannot_read (images.rs:1007, new) writes a TIFF with each of the eight tags through the host ImageMagick (magick_tiff, images.rs:984) and asserts the turned pixels, no tag left in JPEG, PNG and WebP output, orient() after a custom step turning once, and under the opt-out the sensor's pixels, the orientation-only EXIF holding the tag in JPEG, PNG and WebP (taken from ImageMagick's own reading by orientation_args and parse_orientation, magick.rs:1273-1299, used at magick.rs:876-886), orient() turning it, and orient() after a custom step turning it, where the tag crosses the PNG between two runs as ImageMagick's orNT chunk read by metadata::magick_png_orientation (metadata.rs:909-914, called from rust_stage at magick.rs:762-766; 651436b0b states that this orient() left the TIFF unturned while the step read only eXIf); this closes the draft's gap that the unread-format path ran only as argv. HEIC itself is not run; it takes the same detected-None path. Selection: scripts/check-images.sh (unchanged since the draft) runs cargo nextest run -p suprnova --lib --test media --test memory -E 'test(/img_00[0-9]_/)' --run-ignored all; every test named here matches that filter (the media binary declares pub mod images at framework/tests/media/main.rs:9, the memory binary pub mod images at framework/tests/memory/main.rs:17, the lib tests sit in #[cfg(test)] mod tests of framework/src/media), framework/tests/media/images.rs and framework/tests/memory/images.rs are #![cfg(feature = "media")] and media is a default feature (framework/Cargo.toml:288-300), the magick-half tests are #[ignore = "requires a host ImageMagick 7 binary"] and run under --run-ignored all, and they fail rather than skip without the binary (MagickCliDriver::from_env then process returns an error that unwrap turns into a panic; the script also exits 1 first when magick is not on PATH).
requirement IMG-001 Q4 observed: The 2026-10-06 amendment (escalation 5ef445fa) brought the requirement text in line with the code: the draft's defect, that the text said -auto-orient while magick applies fixed transforms for the five formats the framework reads, is resolved by the text, which manual/images.md:137-146 already matched. Not checked: HEIC, which takes the same -auto-orient and probe path as TIFF but has no fixture, and the host ImageMagick's HEIC delegate is not required by scripts/check-images.sh. The orientation probe added for a format the framework cannot read (orientation_args, magick.rs:1273-1282) runs ImageMagick a second time on the whole input under the opt-out in process (magick.rs:876-886) and on every dimensions call with orientation on (magick.rs:946-953), a cost no test measures; parse_orientation (magick.rs:1284-1299) and magick_png_orientation (metadata.rs:909-914, an orNT of other than one byte falls back to eXIf) have no unit test and run only through the TIFF test. ImageDriver::dimensions under both drivers reports a quarter-turned source with its sides swapped and dominant_color skips the turn (oxideav.rs:663-681, magick.rs:941-968), checked for tag 6 only by img_001_both_drivers_report_a_tagged_source_at_its_turned_size (images.rs:1815, now also a tag-6 TIFF with and without the opt-out) and img_001_dimensions_and_dominant_color_do_not_turn_the_image (memory/images.rs:1217). The magick driver's reading of the tag inflates nothing in Rust but is not measured. New public surface with no test of its own: OxideAvImageDriver::decoded_dimensions (oxideav.rs:367, #[doc(hidden)], exists for the memory test) and the pub field ImageConfig::auto_orient. framework/Cargo.toml:267-268 adds image =0.25.10 and moxcms =0.8.1 under the media feature. Docs changed with no check beyond img_008's substring test: manual/images.md Orientation section (130-177, which does not say that under the opt-out a TIFF's tag in the output is ImageMagick's reading), manual/env-vars.md:424, manual/parity.md:102, CHANGELOG.md 'Images are upright and carry no metadata'; no CHANGELOG line names the TIFF fixes of 51099e33d and 651436b0b.
requirement IMG-002 Q3 observed: (1) No Exif or XMP APP1, APP13, COM, PNG eXIf, tEXt, zTXt, iTXt or tIME, or WebP EXIF or XMP chunk other than the opt-out's Orientation-only EXIF: oxideav encodes from pixels and adds only what Kept::prepare (framework/src/media/metadata.rs:1142) and metadata::add (metadata.rs:1245) insert, the EXIF being orientation_only_exif (framework/src/media/orientation.rs:101); magick passes the fixed +profile '!icc,*' (output_args, framework/src/media/magick.rs:1213-1214) and MagickCliDriver::settle (magick.rs:559-624) strips its output in place with metadata::strip (metadata.rs:1417) through keeps_jpeg_segment (1363), drops_png_chunk (1376), strip_webp (1484) and strip_gif (1521). img_002_oxideav_output_keeps_no_exif_xmp_iptc_or_text and img_002_magick_output_keeps_no_exif_xmp_iptc_or_text (framework/tests/media/images.rs:1234, 1241) send JPEG, PNG and WebP sources carrying EXIF with GPS, XMP, IPTC, COM, tEXt, zTXt, iTXt and tIME to JPEG, PNG, WebP, GIF and BMP and assert metadata_found (images.rs:525) is empty; img_001_magick_orients_a_format_the_framework_cannot_read (images.rs:1007) now also runs the '!icc,!exif,*' keep of a format the framework cannot read end to end and asserts only the orientation-only EXIF survives. (5) A flat Display P3 GIF within one step of its sRGB conversion: gif_to_srgb (metadata.rs:1047); img_002_oxideav_gif_output_is_srgb and img_002_magick_gif_output_is_srgb (images.rs:1512, 1519) compare every pixel with moxcms's conversion of the decoded colour, tolerance 1. (6) oxideav never allocates past IMAGE_MAX_ALLOC_BYTES inflating an iCCP: find_profile inflates only the 128-byte header with inflate_prefix (metadata.rs:427), finish charges pixels plus twice the declared size before FoundProfile::read (metadata.rs:829) inflates into an exact buffer with inflate_to (metadata.rs:491); img_002_a_png_icc_profile_inflates_within_the_budget and img_002_a_profile_and_its_copy_are_charged_to_the_budget (framework/tests/memory/images.rs:999, 1059) measure the dhat peak, and img_002_an_inflate_stops_one_byte_past_the_size_it_checks (metadata.rs:1642). (7) Colour chunks kept with the profile, dropped when it is converted away: png_colour_chunks (metadata.rs:400) is copied only when the profile stays (oxideav.rs:505-527; magick's settle at magick.rs:616-620); img_002_oxideav_keeps_png_colour_chunks_with_their_profile and _magick_ (images.rs:1581, 1588, through assert_png_colour_chunks_are_kept at images.rs:1539) now feed cHRM, gAMA and a cICP (P3_CICP, images.rs:1535) with a Display P3 profile and assert all three come out byte for byte in order, and sRGB alone without a profile; img_002_oxideav_never_puts_a_grey_profile_on_rgb and _magick_ (through assert_a_grey_profile_never_describes_rgb, images.rs:1348) now feed gAMA, cHRM and cICP with a grey profile and assert, with colour_chunks_of (images.rs:492), that all three stay exactly when the profile stays and none is left when it is converted away; img_002_a_colour_chunk_is_kept_only_at_its_defined_length (metadata.rs:1725) covers malformed lengths. This closes the draft's cICP and converted-away gaps; sRGB is not fed beside a profile, which PNG forbids, and the keep or drop is one decision for the whole list. Selection: scripts/check-images.sh (unchanged since the draft) runs cargo nextest run -p suprnova --lib --test media --test memory -E 'test(/img_00[0-9]_/)' --run-ignored all; every test named here matches that filter (the media binary declares pub mod images at framework/tests/media/main.rs:9, the memory binary pub mod images at framework/tests/memory/main.rs:17, the lib tests sit in #[cfg(test)] mod tests of framework/src/media), framework/tests/media/images.rs and framework/tests/memory/images.rs are #![cfg(feature = "media")] and media is a default feature (framework/Cargo.toml:288-300), the magick-half tests are #[ignore = "requires a host ImageMagick 7 binary"] and run under --run-ignored all, and they fail rather than skip without the binary (MagickCliDriver::from_env then process returns an error that unwrap turns into a panic; the script also exits 1 first when magick is not on PATH).
requirement IMG-002 Q4 observed: Beyond the tests: the default driver's JPEG encoder writes JFIF YCbCr (jpeg_frame, framework/src/media/oxideav.rs:1291), changing every JPEG it writes; only img_002_a_jpeg_decodes_to_the_same_colours_in_libjpeg (framework/tests/media/images.rs:1627) checks it, against the host libjpeg. The magick handling of formats the framework cannot read ('!icc,!exif,*' in intermediate_args and output_args, framework/src/media/magick.rs:735-744, 1213-1214) is checked as argv by img_002_the_arguments_that_keep_metadata_are_fixed_strings (magick.rs:2069) and now end to end for a TIFF only, not HEIC. strip_bmp_profile (framework/src/media/metadata.rs:1540) and keeps_gif_block (metadata.rs:1388) are reached only with JPEG, PNG and WebP sources, never with a GIF source carrying comment or XMP application extensions or a BMP source with a V5 profile under magick. magick refuses a PNG whose iCCP length differs from its header (libpng), where oxideav processes it without the profile; the test tolerates that difference. The refusals for an oversized profile ImageMagick writes (settle, magick.rs:570-579) and for a profile between two runs (rust_stage, magick.rs:768-778), and the internal error after a second mismatch (magick.rs:924-929), have no test. drops_png_chunk (metadata.rs:1376) drops ImageMagick's orNT and Raw profile chunks with no direct test of a magick PNG carrying a Raw profile chunk; orNT is not in the falsifier's list. metadata::add inserts into ImageMagick's output with Vec::splice (insert, metadata.rs:1234-1242, from settle at magick.rs:622), unmeasured. Docs: manual/images.md Metadata section (179-218), CHANGELOG.md.
requirement IMG-003 Q3 observed: (1) An image that chooses a driver is processed by it: Image::using (framework/src/media/mod.rs:673) stores an ImageDriverKind in the image's own driver field (mod.rs:424), and Image::blocking (mod.rs:684-701) resolves it through driver_of (mod.rs:370-375) before falling back to default_driver (mod.rs:347). img_003_an_image_can_choose_its_driver (framework/tests/media/images.rs:1887) reruns the binary with IMAGE_MAGICK_BINARY naming a missing binary (MISSING_MAGICK, images.rs:1881) and IMAGE_DRIVER removed, and its child img_003_an_image_can_choose_its_driver_child (images.rs:1900) asserts that the image choosing ImageDriverKind::Magick fails with an error containing that name while the same image without the choice succeeds. New since the draft, img_003_an_image_can_choose_oxideav_over_a_magick_default (images.rs:1933) reruns the binary with IMAGE_DRIVER=magick and the missing binary, and its child img_003_an_image_can_choose_oxideav_over_a_magick_default_child (images.rs:1948) asserts that the image without a choice fails naming the missing binary while the same image with using(ImageDriverKind::OxideAv) succeeds with exactly the bytes OxideAvImageDriver::process writes for that pipeline; this closes the draft's gap that only the magick direction was shown. The unit test img_003_a_driver_choice_belongs_to_the_image_that_made_it (mod.rs:1284) checks the field on the image and its clones and that driver_of returns drivers named oxideav and magick. A choice over a custom default installed with set_default_driver (mod.rs:386) is still not driven. (2) The choice does not change another image or the process default: the first child asserts the original image and a second Image::from_bytes succeed on the default and that suprnova::media::default_driver().name() is still oxideav; the second asserts the default is still magick and the image without the choice still fails; the unit test asserts the original keeps driver None. Each _child returns at once unless SUPRNOVA_TEST_OWN_PROCESS is set, and its parent fails unless own_process::assert_child_passed sees one passed test. Selection: all five tests match test(/img_00[0-9]_/) in --test media and --lib of scripts/check-images.sh, none is #[ignore], none needs ImageMagick, and media is a default feature. Since 8f29328e1, images.rs img_003_an_image_choice_overrides_a_custom_default (own process: a custom set_default_driver default, then using(oxideav) gives the built-in bytes and using(magick) with a missing binary names it) and img_003_an_image_can_choose_the_host_magick_over_a_custom_default (ignored, run by the images mechanism with --run-ignored all) cover a choice over a custom default; ignoring using() under a custom default fails both.
requirement IMG-003 Q4 observed: driver_of builds each built-in driver once per process in OXIDEAV_DRIVER and MAGICK_DRIVER (framework/src/media/mod.rs:313-314, 370-375), apart from DEFAULT_DRIVER (mod.rs:312), so an image that chooses a built-in driver bypasses a custom driver installed with set_default_driver (mod.rs:386); no test covers that interaction, using() with the from_path, from_disk, from_upload or from_stream sources, or a chosen driver through the terminals other than to_bytes (to_response, save, store, store_as, dimensions, width, height, mime_type, dominant_color, to_base64, to_data_uri), which all go through the same blocking() (mod.rs:684-701). ImageDriverKind is part of the Image's Clone and Debug state. Docs: manual/images.md mentions using( (checked only as a substring by img_008_the_manual_documents_the_image_api), manual/parity.md:102, CHANGELOG.md 'The rest of Laravel's Image API'.
requirement IMG-004 Q3 observed: (1) A custom transformation registered without an ImageDriver impl applies at its place under both drivers: register_transformation (framework/src/media/custom.rs:166) stores a pixel function by name; oxideav's Transformation::Custom arm (framework/src/media/oxideav.rs:1104) calls custom() (oxideav.rs:1016-1026) in pipeline order; magick splits the pipeline at custom steps with custom_stages (framework/src/media/magick.rs:715-733) and runs each in rust_stage (magick.rs:754-822) between two ImageMagick runs in run_custom_steps (magick.rs:489-514). img_004_a_custom_step_runs_at_its_place_under_oxideav and _under_magick (framework/tests/media/images.rs:2053, 2060) run a resize, a custom invert and a flop and assert the step saw exactly the resized pixels and the output is the mirrored inverse; img_004_a_custom_step_runs_once_when_magick_runs_again (images.rs:1851) asserts one call when the tail reruns; new since the draft, img_004_a_custom_step_can_change_the_size_within_the_limits_under_oxideav and _under_magick (images.rs:2144, 2151, through assert_a_custom_step_can_change_the_size at images.rs:2108) register a step that doubles the width and assert the following flop works on the widened image, and with IMAGE_MAX_DIMENSION one below the new width that the pipeline fails naming the size and IMAGE_MAX_DIMENSION, a refusal only sniff::enforce_limits writes (framework/src/media/sniff.rs:175-181), so dropping its call from custom() (oxideav.rs:1024) or rust_stage (magick.rs:783) fails the test; this closes the draft's size-change gap (the IMAGE_MAX_ALLOC_BYTES branch of the same enforce_limits is not driven through a custom step); unit tests img_004_a_registered_transformation_receives_the_pixels (custom.rs:199) and img_004_custom_steps_split_the_pipeline_and_add_no_argument (magick.rs:2116). (2) It receives the image's pixels at its place: the two _runs_at_its_place tests compare what the probe recorded with the prefix pipeline's own output. (3) The magick argv carries nothing it supplied: transformation_args returns no argument for Custom (magick.rs:1094) and intermediate_args is fixed (magick.rs:735-744); img_004_a_magick_custom_step_adds_no_argument_and_no_file (images.rs:2159) runs its _child (images.rs:2182) with a wrapper binary that logs argv and asserts two runs, no argument containing the step's name, a second run reading png:-[0], and, new, no argument containing a slash, so none names a file. (4) No temporary file for a magick pipeline with a custom step: run (magick.rs:145-278) passes the image over stdin and stdout; the parent points TMPDIR and MAGICK_TEMPORARY_PATH at directories of its own, the wrapper now lists both as each run starts, and the child asserts that listing is empty and that both directories are empty once the pipeline is done; this closes the draft's gap that only TMPDIR was read. A file the driver wrote and removed within one Rust stage, or wrote in a third directory, would still not be seen. (5) The pipeline keeps the source's ICC profile and the opt-out's Orientation tag: rust_stage re-encodes the intermediate PNG with an RGB profile (magick.rs:785-821) and settle re-adds the framework-read tag (magick.rs:908, 613-622); img_004_a_custom_step_keeps_the_profile_and_the_tag_under_oxideav and _under_magick (images.rs:2327, 2334) assert the Display P3 profile and only the Orientation-only EXIF in JPEG, PNG and WebP, the magick child asserts the same for JPEG, img_001_orient_after_a_custom_step_* and img_002_*_never_puts_a_grey_profile_on_rgb also run custom steps, and img_001_magick_orients_a_format_the_framework_cannot_read (images.rs:1007) asserts a TIFF's tag value after a custom step under the opt-out. (6) An unregistered transformation fails naming it: CustomTransformation::apply (custom.rs:132-141); img_004_an_unregistered_step_fails_naming_it_under_oxideav and _under_magick (images.rs:2083, 2090) and img_004_an_unregistered_transformation_fails_naming_it (custom.rs:188). The _child returns at once unless SUPRNOVA_TEST_OWN_PROCESS is set and the parent asserts it ran and passed; both are #[ignore] and run under --run-ignored all. Selection: scripts/check-images.sh (unchanged since the draft) runs cargo nextest run -p suprnova --lib --test media --test memory -E 'test(/img_00[0-9]_/)' --run-ignored all; every test named here matches that filter (the media binary declares pub mod images at framework/tests/media/main.rs:9, the memory binary pub mod images at framework/tests/memory/main.rs:17, the lib tests sit in #[cfg(test)] mod tests of framework/src/media), framework/tests/media/images.rs and framework/tests/memory/images.rs are #![cfg(feature = "media")] and media is a default feature (framework/Cargo.toml:288-300), the magick-half tests are #[ignore = "requires a host ImageMagick 7 binary"] and run under --run-ignored all, and they fail rather than skip without the binary (MagickCliDriver::from_env then process returns an error that unwrap turns into a panic; the script also exits 1 first when magick is not on PATH).
requirement IMG-004 Q4 observed: Still untested: an error returned by the registered function itself, re-registration replacing a function (framework/src/media/custom.rs:166-175), a custom step's result refused for IMAGE_MAX_ALLOC_BYTES rather than IMAGE_MAX_DIMENSION, and a third-party ImageDriver that runs Transformation::Custom through CustomTransformation::apply as the ImageDriver docs describe (framework/src/media/driver.rs:245-249). Under magick each custom step adds an ImageMagick run and a PNG round trip through Rust (decode_unoriented, then an RGBA encode, magick.rs:754-822); the input copy the draft named (payload = input.to_vec()) is gone, the stdin writer now borrowing the input from a scoped thread (magick.rs:226-245). For a format the framework cannot read under the opt-out, the tag crosses the PNG between runs only as ImageMagick's orNT chunk or eXIf, read by metadata::magick_png_orientation (framework/src/media/metadata.rs:909-914), now reached by img_001_magick_orients_a_format_the_framework_cannot_read for TIFF only. The registry is a process-global RwLock map (custom.rs:144-148). ImagePixels, CustomTransformation, Transformation::custom and register_transformation are new public surface listed in suprnova-cli/src/registry/scan/allowlist.jsonl. Docs: manual/images.md Custom transformations section (220-266), CHANGELOG.md.
requirement IMG-005 Q3 observed: (1) The returned path reads back the processed bytes: Image::store (framework/src/media/mod.rs:760-769) runs the pipeline once in processed_with_format, names the file and writes it with put_on_disk (mod.rs:894), returning the path it wrote, and store_as (mod.rs:775-783) does the same under a given name; img_005_store_and_store_as_return_the_path_they_wrote (framework/tests/media/images.rs:2350, #[cfg(feature = "testing")], a default feature) registers a memory disk under Storage::fake and asserts disk.get of the first stored path equals the same pipeline's to_bytes and disk.get("avatars/me.png") equals to_bytes for store_as. (2) A generated name is 40 random alphanumerics plus the output format's extension: generated_name (mod.rs:870-877) samples rand::distr::Alphanumeric 40 times and resolve_format (mod.rs:843) gives the extension; is_generated (images.rs:2340) checks both webp outputs and the png extension of a pipeline that keeps the source format, and the unit test img_005_a_stored_path_joins_directory_and_name (mod.rs:1299, #[cfg(feature = "filesystem")]) checks length, alphabet and that two names differ; randomness is checked only by names differing. (3) Two images in one directory get different names: assert_ne!(first, second) in the same test. (4) store_as writes exactly dir/name: stored_path (mod.rs:882-889) joins with the slashes at the ends trimmed; the test asserts the returned path is avatars/me.png and reads it back, and the unit test covers trimming and an empty directory. (5) Today's store(disk, path) form fails to compile: store now takes (directory: &str, disk: Option<&str>); img_005_and_img_008_the_breaking_forms_do_not_compile (images.rs:2398) runs trybuild over framework/tests/media/compile_fail/, whose store_disk_path.stderr pins E0308 'expected Option<&str>, found &str'. Selection: all three tests match test(/img_00[0-9]_/) in --test media or --lib of scripts/check-images.sh, none is #[ignore], none needs ImageMagick, and filesystem and testing are default features (framework/Cargo.toml:288-300).
requirement IMG-005 Q4 observed: store and store_as with disk None go to Storage::default_disk (put_on_disk, framework/src/media/mod.rs:894-906), which no test reaches; store on an unrecognised source without to_format errors in resolve_format (mod.rs:843-861), untested; both methods are #[cfg(feature = "filesystem")], so they disappear without that default feature; stored_path trims the slashes at both ends of the directory and the name, so store_as("a/", "/b.png") writes a/b.png; the extension of a JPEG output (OutputFormat::extension) is not exercised, only webp and png. The old store(disk, path) returned (); a caller that discarded its result now fails to compile, as the compile-fail case shows. Nothing here changed since the draft. Docs: manual/images.md Storage section (377-412) and the CHANGELOG.md breaking-change entry 'Image::store(disk, path) is replaced by store(directory, disk)'.
requirement IMG-006 Q3 observed: (1) Default corners and flattened transparency white in JPEG and GIF from either driver: metadata::default_background (framework/src/media/metadata.rs:1570) gives Color::WHITE for JPEG and GIF; oxideav's Rotate arm records the background in Steps::flatten_onto (framework/src/media/oxideav.rs:1068-1075) and finish flattens with flatten_rgba (oxideav.rs:529-531, framework/src/media/color.rs:137) when metadata::flattens (metadata.rs:1581); magick writes -background from the typed value in transformation_args (framework/src/media/magick.rs:1068-1079) and flattens with -background, -alpha remove, -alpha off in output_args (magick.rs:1201-1208) onto flatten_background (magick.rs:826-840). img_006_oxideav_fills_corners_and_flattens_by_format and img_006_magick_fills_corners_and_flattens_by_format (framework/tests/media/images.rs:2521, 2528) check the corner of a 45 degree turn and a flattened half-transparent image, within 8 levels for JPEG and exactly for GIF. (2) Default corners fully transparent in PNG, WebP and BMP: the same tests assert alpha 0 at (0,0) for Png, WebP and Bmp, and img_006_corners_default_to_what_the_format_can_hold (metadata.rs:1787). (3) A given colour used for the corners and the flatten: the same tests use Color::rgb(0, 0, 255) for corners in all five formats and a quarter turn with that background for the JPEG and GIF flatten; img_006_the_facade_takes_a_background_colour (images.rs:2534) runs Image::rotate_with_background with Color::from_hex("#00ff00"); img_006_flatten_composites_over_the_background_put_over_white (color.rs:181). (4) A colour reaches magick only as the driver's formatting: Transformation::Rotate carries Option<Color> (framework/src/media/driver.rs:157-164) and Color::to_hex (color.rs:107) formats its four bytes; img_006_a_colour_reaches_magick_as_the_drivers_formatting (magick.rs:2137) asserts #aabbccff for a colour parsed from #ABC and no argument containing that text, with img_006_the_driver_writes_the_colour_from_its_bytes and img_006_hex_colours_parse_in_every_documented_form (color.rs:172, 158). Selection: scripts/check-images.sh (unchanged since the draft) runs cargo nextest run -p suprnova --lib --test media --test memory -E 'test(/img_00[0-9]_/)' --run-ignored all; every test named here matches that filter (the media binary declares pub mod images at framework/tests/media/main.rs:9, the memory binary pub mod images at framework/tests/memory/main.rs:17, the lib tests sit in #[cfg(test)] mod tests of framework/src/media), framework/tests/media/images.rs and framework/tests/memory/images.rs are #![cfg(feature = "media")] and media is a default feature (framework/Cargo.toml:288-300), the magick-half tests are #[ignore = "requires a host ImageMagick 7 binary"] and run under --run-ignored all, and they fail rather than skip without the binary (MagickCliDriver::from_env then process returns an error that unwrap turns into a panic; the script also exits 1 first when magick is not on PATH).
requirement IMG-006 Q4 observed: WebPLossless output's transparent default is not exercised (the tests use OutputFormat::WebP); a translucent named background, which the flatten first puts over white (Color::over_white at framework/src/media/color.rs:121, flatten_background at framework/src/media/magick.rs:826-840), is tested only by the flatten_rgba unit test, not through either driver; a pipeline with several rotations, where the last rotation's background is the flatten colour (Steps::flatten_onto at framework/src/media/oxideav.rs:1073, the rev().find_map in flatten_background), is untested. GIF output from either driver has its transparency flattened (metadata::flattens covers GIF, and magick passes -alpha remove for it), a change to transparent GIF sources that no test feeds; mem_audit_image_output_is_byte_for_byte_what_it_was (framework/tests/memory/image_outputs.rs:325) leaves transparent GIFs out on purpose. Transformation::Rotate changed from Rotate(f32) to Rotate { degrees, background }, a breaking change for downstream matches noted in CHANGELOG.md; Color (rgb, rgba, from_hex, to_hex, to_rgba, the channel getters and WHITE, BLACK, TRANSPARENT) is new public surface listed in suprnova-cli/src/registry/scan/allowlist.jsonl. Nothing here changed since the draft. Docs: manual/images.md Operations section.
requirement IMG-007 Q3 observed: (1) None is missing: flip, flop, optimize, to_webp, to_jpg, to_jpeg, to_png, to_gif, to_bmp, to_base64, to_data_uri, width and height are defined in framework/src/media/mod.rs (578, 584, 634, 639-667, 730, 739, 807, 813), and the tests that call them would not compile without them: img_007_shortcuts_record_what_to_format_and_quality_record (mod.rs:1255) and img_007_the_terminals_agree_with_to_bytes_and_dimensions (framework/tests/media/images.rs:2549). (2) to_base64 decodes to to_bytes: the integration test decodes with base64's standard engine and compares. (3) to_data_uri carries mime_type() and that payload: compared with format!("data:{mime};base64,{encoded}"). (4) width and height equal dimensions(): compared. (5) A shortcut or optimize records the same format and quality as to_format and quality: the unit test compares the ImagePipeline of all six shortcuts with to_format and of optimize(WebP, 55) with to_format(WebP).quality(55). (6) flip and flop mirror on Laravel's axes: the unit test compares their pipelines with flip_vertically and flip_horizontally, and the integration test compares the encoded outputs and asserts flip differs from flop; the axis of FlipHorizontally is pinned by img_004_a_custom_step_runs_at_its_place_* (images.rs:2053, 2060, a left-to-right mirror checked against the independent turned(..., 2)) and now also by img_004_a_custom_step_can_change_the_size_within_the_limits_* (images.rs:2144, 2151), so a flop on the other axis fails there, while FlipVertically's axis is only pinned by differing from that mirror. Selection: both IMG-007 tests match test(/img_00[0-9]_/) in --lib and --test media of scripts/check-images.sh, neither is #[ignore], and the integration test runs on the default driver, oxideav.
requirement IMG-007 Q4 observed: width() and height() each run the whole pipeline and then driver.dimensions on its output (framework/src/media/mod.rs:797-815), so each call decodes and encodes again, which no test measures; to_data_uri resolves the MIME type from the target or the sniffed source (resolve_mime, mod.rs:910) and fails for an unrecognised source without to_format, untested; optimize clamps quality to 1..=100 through quality() (mod.rs:628-637), so a quality of 0 becomes 1, untested; to_base64 and to_data_uri are exercised under oxideav only; to_jpg and to_jpeg both record OutputFormat::Jpeg. Nothing here changed since the draft. Docs: manual/images.md Terminals and Operations sections, CHANGELOG.md 'The rest of Laravel's Image API'.
requirement IMG-008 Q3 observed: (1) A struct literal of ImageConfig does not compile outside the crate: #[non_exhaustive] on ImageConfig (framework/src/media/mod.rs:172); framework/tests/media/compile_fail/config_literal.rs, whose .stderr pins E0639. (2) An exhaustive match on Transformation does not compile outside the crate: #[non_exhaustive] (framework/src/media/driver.rs:107); compile_fail/exhaustive_match.rs names all 17 variants without a wildcard and its .stderr pins E0004. Both run in img_005_and_img_008_the_breaking_forms_do_not_compile (framework/tests/media/images.rs:2398) through trybuild. (3) manual/images.md documents orientation, metadata stripping and each method IMG-003 to IMG-007 adds, (4) IMAGE_AUTO_ORIENT appears in manual/images.md and manual/env-vars.md, and (5) the no-argument-position promise stays: img_008_the_manual_documents_the_image_api (images.rs:2598) include_str!s both pages and asserts the headings '## Orientation' and '## Metadata', the whitespace-normalised sentence 'There is no argument position user input can reach.', IMAGE_AUTO_ORIENT, the spellings orient(), using(, transform(, register_transformation, .store(, store_as(, rotate_with_background(, to_base64(), to_data_uri(), width(), height(), to_webp(), to_jpg(), to_jpeg(), to_png(), to_gif(), to_bmp(), optimize(, flip() and flop(), and the backticked `IMAGE_AUTO_ORIENT` in env-vars.md; all are present on main (manual/images.md:130, 179, 156, 594-595; manual/env-vars.md:424). These are substring checks, so a heading or a spelling in an unrelated sentence satisfies them. Selection: both tests match test(/img_00[0-9]_/) in --test media of scripts/check-images.sh, neither is #[ignore], neither needs ImageMagick.
requirement IMG-008 Q4 observed: Changed with no check in this mechanism: CHANGELOG.md ('The image types changed shape', 'The rest of Laravel's Image API', and the new 'Images are no longer copied before a driver reads them'), manual/parity.md:102, feature-map/surface.jsonl and feature-map/laravel/parity.jsonl, and suprnova-cli/src/registry/scan/allowlist.jsonl, which lists suprnova::Color, CustomTransformation, Image::flip and the rest of the new API. The ImageDriver docs (framework/src/media/driver.rs:245-249) tell a custom driver to return an error from its wildcard arm, untested for a third-party driver; OutputFormat stays exhaustive by design (driver.rs:33) and ImagePipeline remains a public struct without #[non_exhaustive]. The manual's orientation text (manual/images.md:137-146) matches the amended IMG-001 but only its heading and the IMAGE_AUTO_ORIENT spelling are checked. The .stderr files pin rustc's exact diagnostic text, so a toolchain change can fail img_005_and_img_008_the_breaking_forms_do_not_compile with no API change.
requirement MEM-003 Q3 observed: The first command of .sudus/mechanisms/mem-footprint.json selects test(/mem_audit/) in the libs of suprnova, suprnova-live, suprnova-magnetar (with oauth), suprnova-macros and suprnova-cli and in the memory binaries (framework/tests/memory and crates/suprnova-live/tests/memory.rs), action_dispatch and upload_file_provider; no nextest default-filter exists (.config/nextest.toml), none of the tests below is #[ignore] (ignored tests do not run there), and the cfg(unix) ones run here. Streaming a 4 MiB file: mem_audit_streaming_a_file_allocates_once_per_chunk (framework/tests/memory/http.rs:28, under 1.5 times the file). Initial Inertia visit with a 1 MiB prop: mem_audit_the_first_inertia_page_is_built_in_one_buffer (http.rs:51, the HTML visit under the XHR visit plus the prop), and, new, mem_audit_the_first_page_url_is_the_path_the_request_built (framework/src/inertia/response.rs:2656), which asserts that page_url (response.rs:2571-2576, called at 1093) returns at the host root the very String the request built for its path and query, and the resolver's String when it keeps its bytes (same address), and one exact buffer under /billing; prefixed_owned and rooted_owned (framework/src/routing/root.rs:218-237) do it. This closes the draft's defect, that the first page's URL was copied at the host root. Wildcard CORS: mem_audit_a_wildcard_cors_path_is_compiled_once (http.rs:104, 200 requests rather than 1,000, fewer than one extra block per request). CSRF exemption rules: mem_audit_normalizing_a_path_borrows_it (framework/src/csrf/middleware.rs:1549). Same address: Context::push in mem_audit_push_onto_a_scalar_moves_the_scalar (framework/src/context/mod.rs:1330) and mem_audit_pushing_onto_an_array_does_not_copy_the_value (framework/tests/memory/jobs.rs:14, by bytes), the job payload in mem_audit_the_job_receives_the_payload_itself (jobs.rs:77), the last broadcast channel in mem_audit_the_last_channel_gets_the_payload_itself (framework/src/broadcasting/broadcastable.rs:440), a pure action argument in mem_audit_prepared_arguments_keep_the_given_values (crates/suprnova-live/tests/action_dispatch.rs:479), a full upload read in mem_audit_a_full_read_returns_the_store_bytes (crates/suprnova-live/tests/upload_file_provider.rs:2090). No extra copy: SQS decode and retry in mem_audit_an_sqs_message_is_parsed_once and mem_audit_a_retried_sqs_request_does_not_copy_the_body (framework/tests/memory/sqs.rs:127, 161); runtime cast and eager load in mem_audit_a_runtime_cast_does_not_copy_its_column and mem_audit_eager_loading_reads_keys_not_rows (framework/tests/memory/data.rs:123, 88); image steps in mem_audit_an_image_pipeline_moves_its_planes (framework/tests/memory/images.rs:26), mem_audit_a_filter_step_moves_planes (framework/src/media/oxideav.rs:2197), mem_audit_an_image_with_a_profile_is_written_in_one_buffer (memory/images.rs:1166, now PNG, JPEG, lossless WebP and lossy WebP output against an allowance of half the output, for the in-place insert of metadata::add, framework/src/media/metadata.rs:1245, which closes the draft's WebP gap), and, new, mem_audit_an_image_from_bytes_reads_the_callers_bytes (memory/images.rs:69: read_source hands the driver the caller's Bytes, framework/src/media/mod.rs:1040-1081, and a 4 MiB BMP through Image::from_bytes must allocate less than half its size more than the driver alone and write the same bytes; 9ad08e09b measured 4,194,614 bytes more before the fix) and mem_audit_the_magick_driver_writes_its_input_without_copying_it (memory/images.rs:1261, cfg(unix): a stand-in binary reads all of stdin and answers a 1x1 PNG, and the driver must allocate under a quarter of a 4 MiB input; the writer borrows the input in a scoped thread with the driver's own read end of the stdin pipe, framework/src/media/magick.rs:160-161, 235-245), guarded by mem_audit_a_child_that_reads_none_of_its_input_does_not_hold_the_call (magick.rs:1976, cfg(unix)), which closes the draft's magick input-copy gap; signed snapshot, composite header and document path in mem_audit_a_mount_does_not_copy_its_signed_snapshot, mem_audit_composite_header_bytes_do_not_clone_the_graph and mem_audit_a_document_path_is_validated_without_copies (crates/suprnova-live/tests/memory.rs:164, 279, 308), mem_audit_a_composite_shell_holds_only_its_literal_bytes (framework/src/render_cache/middleware.rs:4450) and, for reflected_target (framework/src/live/ports/response.rs:241-279), mem_audit_a_reflected_url_at_the_host_root_borrows_the_document_path (response.rs:294); process results in mem_audit_process_output_is_held_once (framework/tests/memory/media.rs:59, cfg(unix)), mem_audit_waiting_moves_the_captured_output (framework/src/process/invoked.rs:1356, cfg(unix)) and mem_audit_valid_utf8_output_is_stored_once (framework/src/process/mod.rs:684); append in mem_audit_appending_does_not_copy_the_file_twice (framework/tests/memory/files.rs:12); hex in mem_audit_hex_is_written_into_one_exact_buffer and mem_audit_no_source_formats_hex_a_byte_at_a_time (crates/suprnova-magnetar/src/storage/mod.rs:161, 176). The last clause, that no output differs from today's outside the listed exceptions: new, mem_audit_image_output_is_byte_for_byte_what_it_was (framework/tests/memory/image_outputs.rs:325, module declared at framework/tests/memory/main.rs:16) hashes 394 outputs, sizes and average colours of the built-in driver and fails on any digest that differs from framework/tests/memory/fixtures/image-outputs-c04577b96.txt or any count mismatch; its sources carry no orientation, profile or metadata, and JPEG outputs and transparent GIFs are left out, which are the IMG-001, IMG-002, IMG-006 and JFIF YCbCr exceptions. Still without a test: magick's output (it carried a timestamp at c04577b96) and every response, frame, digest, snapshot, file and encoded string outside images; each mem_audit test there asserts only its own expected output. Known residual: Live's redirect target. At the host root SuprnovaResponseIntentPort uses the resolved target as it is (prefixed_owned, framework/src/live/ports/response.rs:156), but navigation_target (response.rs:90-106) hands it to EndpointNavigationTarget::parse (crates/suprnova-live/src/endpoint/response.rs:37-48), which copies it with target.to_owned() (response.rs:48), so no test fails if line 156 goes back to prefixed(&target). Since 549df3436, the magick driver's metadata insert no longer reallocates ImageMagick's output: process reserves the most metadata it can add and the last run reads the output into a buffer that keeps that room (framework/src/media/magick.rs), so the insert moves bytes within it; memory/images.rs mem_audit_the_magick_driver_adds_metadata_without_copying_its_output failed before the fix (16,783,686 bytes with the chunk against 8,394,914 without) and fails again with the room set to 0.
requirement MEM-003 Q4 observed: Not covered by mem-footprint: the default driver's JPEG encoder writes JFIF YCbCr (jpeg_frame, framework/src/media/oxideav.rs:1291), changing every JPEG it writes and allocating three planes the size of the old RGB copy, checked only by img_002_a_jpeg_decodes_to_the_same_colours_in_libjpeg in the images mechanism, and left out of the c04577b96 digests as an exception; the magick driver runs ImageMagick once per custom step, again for an sRGB conversion or a profile mismatch, and now once more for an orientation probe of a format the framework cannot read under the opt-out and in dimensions (framework/src/media/magick.rs:876-886, 946-953), and inserts metadata into ImageMagick's output with Vec::splice (insert, framework/src/media/metadata.rs:1234-1242, from settle at magick.rs:622), which reallocates when the output Vec has no spare room, none of it measured; orientation allocates a second plane for every non-identity tag, the mirrors and the half turn included (Orientation::apply, framework/src/media/orientation.rs:72-93), counted by IMG-001's estimate but by no mem_audit test; Image::from_path and from_disk now move their read buffer into Bytes (mod.rs:1046-1079) with no mem_audit test of their own. The Live redirect target at the host root is the residual in Q3: prefixed_owned (framework/src/live/ports/response.rs:156) avoids one copy that EndpointNavigationTarget::parse (crates/suprnova-live/src/endpoint/response.rs:48) makes anyway, untested; root::prefixed (framework/src/routing/root.rs:204-210) still allocates even at the host root for its other callers. The prepend half of 'filesystem append and prepend' and the SQS reservation copy named in the requirement text have no mem_audit test (neither is in the falsifier). The new writer's deadline behaviour is covered only by tests no mechanism selects: the_invocation_is_bounded_in_wall_clock_time (magick.rs:1892) has no mem_audit or img_ name, and an_orphaned_grandchild_cannot_outlive_the_deadline, a_stalled_child_is_killed_at_the_hard_deadline and a_large_payload_round_trips_without_deadlocking (magick.rs:1902, 1939, 1992) are #[ignore]. CHANGELOG.md gained 'Images are no longer copied before a driver reads them'. The requirement text (docs/spec/memory.md:54-77) is Agreed 2026-10-06.
requirement LDB-001 Q3 observed: Clause 1, a framework feature fails on the committed Laravel 13 schema or needs a column it lacks: the stores write only Laravel's columns, DatabaseQueueDriver (framework/src/queue/database.rs:66, job_payload :152, pop_filtered :888), DatabaseBatchRepository (framework/src/queue/batch.rs:468, encode_batch_options :1166), DatabaseFailedJobStore::log (framework/src/queue/failed.rs:399-430), DatabaseSessionDriver (framework/src/session/driver/database.rs:79, encode_payload :127), DatabaseChannel::deliver (framework/src/notifications/channels/database.rs:54), the Pennant store upsert (framework/src/features/store.rs:188) and the spatie statements in framework/src/rbac/has_roles.rs:117-217. tables::ldb_001_queue_session_and_notification_stores_work_on_laravels_tables_{sqlite,postgres,mysql} (framework/tests/laravel_database/tables.rs:456-561) pushes, pops and acks on Laravel's jobs, stores and settles a batch, writes and reads a session and delivers and reads notifications on support::laravel; every failed_jobs case also runs with Origin::Laravel (failed_jobs.rs:26-34, failed_jobs_table :49-61); columns::ldb_006_scaffold_user_finds_updates_and_creates_* covers users; packages::ldb_008_pennant_flags_read_and_write_* and packages::ldb_008_spatie_roles_and_permissions_* cover features, roles and permissions. Clause 2, a table the framework's or scaffold's migrations create on an empty database differs in column, type, nullability, key or index other than DATETIME for TIMESTAMP on MySQL: create_failed_jobs and create_jobs (framework/src/queue/migrations/mod.rs:145, :292), create_batches (:471), sessions_layout (framework/src/session/migrations.rs:108-121), create_table (framework/src/notifications/migrations/m_create_notifications_table.rs:78), create_features (framework/src/features/migrations/m_create_features_table.rs:67), create_named, create_model_has and create_role_has_permissions (framework/src/rbac/migrations/m_create_rbac_tables.rs:65, :82, :107) and suprnova-cli/src/templates/files/backend/migrations/create_users_table.rs.tpl; tables::ldb_001_tables_suprnova_creates_take_laravels_layout_* (tables.rs:104-119) runs the Migrator of framework/tests/laravel_database/scaffold.rs:29-65 (the scaffold templates compiled through #[path] plus the framework migrations) on an empty database and compares the 12 LAYOUT_TABLES (tables.rs:32-45) with assert_same_layout (tables.rs:55-91): column names in order, catalog types with only timestamp to datetime allowed on MySQL (same_kind :50-52), nullability, primary key, named indexes with their columns and uniqueness, and foreign keys, read by catalog.rs. Clause 3, notifiable_id under NOTIFICATIONS_MORPH_KEY int, uuid and ulid: morph_key (m_create_notifications_table.rs:60); tables::ldb_001_notifiable_id_and_user_id_follow_the_key_type_* (tables.rs:138-181) sets NOTIFICATIONS_MORPH_KEY to each value and compares notifications, and sessions built by create_sessions_table with SessionUserKey::Uuid, Ulid and Integer, with Laravel's layout, the keyed column taking laravel_key_kind (tables.rs:123-131). sessions.user_id with an integer-keyed and a unique_id-keyed user model, the draft's open gap, is now closed by tables::ldb_001_sessions_user_id_follows_an_integer_and_a_unique_id_keyed_user_model_{sqlite,postgres,mysql} (tables.rs:389-451, sign_in_with :301-387): it declares three #[model] users, LdbIntUser with a u64 id, LdbUuidUser with unique_id = "uuid" and LdbUlidUser with unique_id = "ulid" (tables.rs:184-216), runs the shipped CreateSessionsTable::new (framework/src/session/migrations.rs:59-67, SessionUserKey :44-54, column choice :111-115) with the key each model's UNIQUE_ID_KIND gives (session_key, tables.rs:241-246), asserts the sessions layout equals Laravel's with user_id of the foreignId, foreignUuid or foreignUlid type, then creates a user through the model, signs it in with Auth::login_using_id behind the loopback Browser, and asserts /whoami answers its key, sessions.user_id holds that key and session::destroy_all_for_user signs it out; a wrong column type fails assert_same_layout, and a column that cannot hold the key fails the user_id equality. Selection: every named test is an ldb_0NN_ function in a module of framework/tests/laravel_database/main.rs, which test(/ldb_0[0-9][0-9]_/) in scripts/check-laravel-database.sh selects; the _postgres and _mysql variants carry #[ignore] from on_every_engine! (framework/tests/laravel_database/support.rs:42-64) or by hand and run because the script passes --run-ignored all and exports PG_TEST_URL and MYSQL_TEST_URL, support::empty panics rather than skips when a URL is unset (support.rs:151-152, 166-167), and no module of the binary is cfg-gated.
requirement LDB-001 Q4 observed: framework/tests/laravel_database/scaffold.rs:45-63 copies the scaffold Migrator by hand; suprnova-cli/src/templates/files/backend/migrations/mod.rs.tpl itself is not compiled by this mechanism (only the ignored scaffold_snapshot test does that), and it does not list CreateJobsTable, CreateJobBatchesTable, CreateFailedJobsTable or the notifications, features and RBAC migrations, which manual/laravel-database.md (Registering the migrations) tells applications to add. The sessions key stays the application's argument: the new LDB-001 test maps each model's UNIQUE_ID_KIND to a SessionUserKey in its own helper (tables.rs:241-246), the scaffold passes SessionUserKey::Integer (create_sessions_table.rs.tpl:18, migrations/mod.rs.tpl:22), and no test covers an application whose user model's key and that argument disagree; the test signs in through its own ModelUsers provider (tables.rs:252-271) over users tables it creates by hand, not through EloquentUserProvider. Reads and writes on the Laravel tables that no test drives: unread_for, read_for, mark_as_read, mark_as_unread, mark_all_as_read and delete_for (framework/src/notifications/database_read.rs:162-280), the features admin facade list, get, upsert and delete (framework/src/features/admin.rs:70-148), framework/src/rbac/gate_bridge.rs and framework/src/rbac/middleware.rs, SESSION_TABLE (sessions_table, framework/src/session/migrations.rs:77) and FEATURES_USER_SCOPE (user_scope, framework/src/features/store.rs:55-61). MariaDB is never run (the script's MySQL is 8.4 on port 3317). The dogfood app's migration chain (app/src/migrations/mod.rs:116-121) has no step here. Outside every check: the manual pages rewritten for the layouts (manual/queues.md, session.md, notifications.md, feature-flags.md, authorization.md, env-vars.md, migrations.md), CHANGELOG.md, feature-map/tools/runtime_surface.py and build_surface.py, and the existing suites rewritten for the new layouts (framework/tests/queue/*, session/*, notifications/*, rbac/*, features/*, auth/scaffold_tables.rs, schema/unsigned_auth.rs), which this mechanism does not run.
requirement LDB-002 Q3 observed: Fresh uuid, never the envelope id, a second dead letter accepted: DatabaseFailedJobStore::log draws Uuid::new_v4 (framework/src/queue/failed.rs:406); failed_jobs::ldb_002_a_dead_letter_gets_a_fresh_uuid_{sqlite,postgres,mysql} (framework/tests/laravel_database/failed_jobs.rs:108-140) logs one envelope twice on a Laravel-created and a migration-created table (ORIGINS, failed_jobs.rs:34) and asserts both uuids differ from env.id and from each other. payload with displayName and uuid: failed_payload (failed.rs:370); ldb_002_the_payload_and_connection_are_what_laravel_reads_* (failed_jobs.rs:145-182) decodes the stored payload and checks displayName, uuid and Envelope::from_json. connection not one of sync, database, beanstalkd, sqs, redis, deferred, background or failover: SUPRNOVA_CONNECTION_PREFIX suprnova: (failed.rs:291, written by log at :418); the same test asserts the stored connection is outside LARAVEL_CONNECTIONS (failed_jobs.rs:38-47). failed_at within a second: now_whole_seconds (failed.rs:390-395); ldb_002_failed_at_is_within_a_second_of_the_failure_* (failed_jobs.rs:185-217). 1 MiB payload and exception: long_text columns (framework/src/queue/migrations/mod.rs:151-152); ldb_002_a_mebibyte_payload_and_exception_are_stored_whole_* (failed_jobs.rs:220-250). The command clauses, the draft's open gap, now run as console commands of a real Application: failed_jobs::console (failed_jobs.rs:260-274) re-runs the test binary with --exact failed_jobs::console_child, whose child (failed_jobs.rs:279-305) builds an Application whose bootstrap connects to the test database and calls run_with_args with the command's argv under QUEUE_DRIVER=database, so the boot binds DatabaseQueueDriver on jobs and DatabaseFailedJobStore on failed_jobs (framework/src/queue/mod.rs:1867-1910); run_cli dispatches the four commands (framework/src/app/mod.rs:1301-1327) to run_failed_jobs_internal (:1928-1946) and framework/src/queue/failed_console.rs (list :89, retry :135-160, forget :165-176, flush with cutoff_before :178-202). ldb_002_failed_lists_retry_pushes_and_forget_deletes_* (failed_jobs.rs:330-405) asserts queue:failed exits 0 and prints a line holding each row's uuid and job name, queue:retry <uuid> prints the pushed message, deletes the row and leaves on jobs an envelope whose id is the original one (popped through DatabaseQueueDriver), and queue:forget <uuid> prints the deleted message and deletes the row, on both origins; ldb_002_flush_hours_deletes_older_rows_and_keeps_newer_* (failed_jobs.rs:409-464) backdates rows by 3 and 1 hours, runs queue:flush --hours=2, asserts the older-than-2-hours message, the 3-hour row gone and the 1-hour row kept, so a wrong --hours to cutoff conversion fails it. queue:retry pushes a row Laravel wrote: Queue::retry_failed maps the Envelope::from_json failure to an error naming the uuid (framework/src/queue/mod.rs:1336-1342), and retry_all_failed skips it (:1394); ldb_002_a_laravel_row_lists_and_retry_refuses_it_* (failed_jobs.rs:469-523) asserts queue:failed lists Laravel's row with App\Jobs\FailingJob and its queue, queue:retry <uuid> exits 1 with the uuid on stderr, jobs keeps its row count and the failed row stays, and queue:retry all exits 0, keeps the row and pushes nothing. Shipped migration against Laravel's stub, DATETIME failed_at on MySQL: create_failed_jobs (queue/migrations/mod.rs:145-157); failed_jobs is one of the 12 tables tables::ldb_001_tables_suprnova_creates_take_laravels_layout_* compares, and tables::ldb_011_tables_suprnova_creates_have_no_timestamp_column_mysql refuses a TIMESTAMP column. ldb_002_capitalized_and_reserved_queue_table_names_work_* (failed_jobs.rs:556-613) adds quoted QUEUE_DB_TABLE and QUEUE_FAILED_DB_TABLE names. The child helper failed_jobs::console_child has no ldb_ prefix, so the filter does not select it on its own, and it returns at once unless LDB_CONSOLE_CHILD is set; a child whose filter matched nothing would print nothing and fail the parent's output assertions. Selection: every named test is an ldb_0NN_ function in a module of framework/tests/laravel_database/main.rs, which test(/ldb_0[0-9][0-9]_/) in scripts/check-laravel-database.sh selects; the _postgres and _mysql variants carry #[ignore] from on_every_engine! (framework/tests/laravel_database/support.rs:42-64) or by hand and run because the script passes --run-ignored all and exports PG_TEST_URL and MYSQL_TEST_URL, support::empty panics rather than skips when a URL is unset (support.rs:151-152, 166-167), and no module of the binary is cfg-gated.
requirement LDB-002 Q4 observed: Beyond what the tests assert: decode_row (framework/src/queue/failed.rs:599-647) strips the suprnova: prefix, keeps a Laravel row's connection as written and reads job_name from displayName, and queue:failed prints that connection (format_listing, framework/src/queue/failed_console.rs:206-248), but no test asserts the connection it reports for either kind of row; the earlier-layout reshape that prefixes the connection and rebuilds the payload (framework/src/queue/migrations/mod.rs:161-233, earlier_failed_payload :244) is reached only through the LDB-010 upgrade tests; MemoryFailedJobStore and NullFailedJobStore (failed.rs:142-275), queue:prune-failed, the no-match and bad-id replies of queue:retry and queue:forget, and the change to framework/src/queue/inspect.rs are not exercised here. The in-source tests of failed_console.rs (:260-489) are lib tests this mechanism does not select. The console children exit 1 themselves when run_with_args returns an error (failed_jobs.rs:301-304), so the exit status of a real binary's queue:retry refusal rests on Application::run and run_cli_or_exit (framework/src/app/mod.rs:1083-1103, 1165-1169), as for LDB-003. No test runs Laravel's own queue:retry against a suprnova: connection; that it refuses rests on the eight names in LARAVEL_CONNECTIONS. Unchecked text and tooling: manual/queues.md, the queue:retry section of manual/laravel-database.md, CHANGELOG.md, and the fixture generator framework/tests/laravel_database/fixtures/generate.sh with fixtures/app (FailingJob.php, BuildFixtures.php), which only regenerate the committed JSON. The rewritten framework/tests/queue/failed_store.rs, retry.rs, database.rs, database_postgres.rs and worker_postgres.rs are outside this mechanism.
requirement LDB-003 Q3 observed: The check runs before the first pop in run_labelled_worker (framework/src/queue/worker.rs:755-757), shared by run_worker (:709, returning Result) and run_worker_on (:738); it is DatabaseFailedJobStore::check with check_failed_jobs_columns (framework/src/queue/failed.rs:534-597): a missing table (does not exist), the earlier layout (envelope_json or job_name present), a written column missing (WRITTEN_COLUMNS :294), and on MySQL a payload or exception whose data_type is not longtext (:582-595). Tests in framework/tests/laravel_database/worker_check.rs: missing table, ldb_003_a_missing_failed_jobs_table_stops_the_worker_{sqlite,postgres,mysql} (:76-91); lacking a written column, ldb_003_a_table_lacking_a_written_column_stops_the_worker_* (:93-115, no exception column); earlier layout, ldb_003_the_earlier_suprnova_layout_stops_the_worker_* (:117-134); narrow MySQL text, ldb_003_a_text_payload_or_exception_stops_the_worker_mysql (:138-163), which uses a TEXT payload and a MEDIUMTEXT exception, so a TEXT exception is covered by the same data_type branch rather than its own case. assert_refused (:51-74) requires run_worker and run_worker_on both to return Err naming failed_jobs and the column and the queue still to hold its job, so a pop or an Ok return fails it. Tables Laravel 13 and the migration created are accepted: ldb_003_laravel_and_migration_tables_are_accepted_* (:165-190). Another store does not refuse: ldb_003_a_worker_without_the_database_store_does_not_refuse_* (:194-213) with MemoryFailedJobStore over an earlier-layout table. queue:work exits non-zero: ldb_003_queue_work_exits_non_zero_when_it_refuses_* (:246-308) runs worker_check::queue_work_child (:313-338) in a child process, which drives queue:work through Application::run_with_args (framework/src/app/mod.rs:1137-1160, run_cli with Failures::Return at :1159) and run_queue_worker_internal (:1782), and asserts exit 1, failed_jobs, envelope_json and the run_with_args returned: marker on stderr and the job left, then exit 0 and the job run on Laravel's table; ldb_003_run_with_args_returns_its_failures_to_the_caller (:484-507) checks that run_with_args returns an unparseable argv and a failed boot precondition as errors and --help as Ok. Residual, argued by construction: the child calls std::process::exit(1) itself when run_with_args returns Err (:333-337); a real binary's Application::run (framework/src/app/mod.rs:1083-1103) calls run_cli_or_exit at :1102, which runs the same run_cli with Failures::Print and calls std::process::exit(1) when it returns an error (:1165-1169), so the tests prove the refusal reaches run_cli as an error and the exit follows from that code, but no selected test runs Application::run or run_cli_or_exit; the in-source tests that do (app::process_exit_tests, framework/src/app/mod.rs:2771, 2984, 3002) are lib tests for other commands that this mechanism does not select. ldb_003_schema_qualified_queue_tables_work_postgres (:352) and ldb_003_schema_qualified_queue_tables_work_mysql (:409) cover schema-qualified table names. queue_work_child has no ldb_ prefix, so the filter does not select it on its own, and it returns at once unless LDB_QUEUE_WORK_CHILD is set. requirement REG-031 Q3 observed: The view scan refuses: include_str! and other macros as view-macro (suprnova-cli/src/registry/scan/view.rs:1453), calls carrying a capability as view-call (view.rs:1063-1072, 1389, 1428), filters that are neither Askama's nor FRAMEWORK_FILTERS as view-filter (:1199, :1232), escape or e with an escaper other than html as view-escape (:1211, :1222), an include, import or extends outside the manifest, dependencies and shipped views as view-include (:739, :893), script, base, meta and the other listed elements as view-element and event handler attributes as view-attribute (suprnova-cli/src/registry/scan/view/markup.rs:535-582), URL attributes through check_url_attribute as view-url (markup.rs:626), and CSS @import and off-origin url() or image-set() as css-import and css-url (suprnova-cli/src/registry/scan/view/css.rs:65-142). The url::root rule: PUBLIC_ROOT (view.rs:96) marks the call (:1078), locals, if-let, match and for keep or drop the mark (:126-175, :815-819, :1261, :1317-1320), a macro argument carrying it is refused (:941-963), and without_roots with rooted_constant (markup.rs:717-755) admits it only before constant text whose first character, after decoding character references and removing tabs and newlines, is one / not followed by / or \. A component installs only if the scan accepts it: live:add resolves its plan with AllowlistScanner (suprnova-cli/src/registry/plan.rs:291-300, resolve :437-444), which calls scan_component_in with the manifest's register, elements and dependency views, and the plan returns RegistryError::Refused on any finding before it writes a file (plan.rs:1100-1113, and :1154-1179 for a kept installed copy). Tests in suprnova-cli/tests/live_registry_scan.rs, run whole by scripts/check-registries-scan.sh with no filter and none marked #[ignore]: reg_022_every_bypass_in_the_corpus_is_refused_with_its_check_file_and_line (:438) requires each component under suprnova-cli/tests/fixtures/registry/bypass/ to be refused with its marked check, file and line, among them view-include-str, view-files-call, view-session-call, view-custom-filter, view-escape-none, view-include-app, view-include-unnamed, view-script, view-base, view-meta-refresh, view-onclick, view-javascript-href, view-form-action, view-protocol-relative, view-template-img, view-style-attr-url, css-import, css-url-remote, css-image-set, css-webkit-image-set and the view-root-* fixtures (view-root-double-slash, view-root-backslash among them), through scan_component and scan_component_with_manifest, which call the same scan_component_in (suprnova-cli/src/registry/scan/mod.rs:103-108, 157-169); reg_031_url_root_before_a_rooted_constant_path_is_admitted (:1123) admits href="{{ suprnova::url::root() }}/x" and five other rooted forms; reg_031_url_root_before_anything_but_a_rooted_constant_is_refused (:1143) refuses //evil.example/x, /\evil.example and 16 more with view-url on line 1; reg_031_url_root_is_admitted_and_carries_no_capability (:1115), reg_031_a_view_may_not_call_a_dependencys_function (:339) and reg_031_the_admitted_framework_filters_match_the_framework (:611) add the capability and filter checks, and reg_016_every_shipped_component_passes_the_view_and_script_scans (:539) and reg_016_every_shipped_component_passes_the_scan (:854) accept the shipped library, whose header-bar.html uses the root inside an if branch. The install clause, the draft's open gap, is now driven by reg_031_live_add_refuses_a_view_that_breaks_a_rule_and_installs_a_clean_one (:1265): it builds a project with a signed vendored library whose key is pinned (project_with_widget, :1183-1238), runs the built suprnova binary's live:add --manifest <manifest> --yes (CARGO_BIN_EXE_suprnova, :1243-1251), and asserts views holding an onclick attribute, a javascript: href, a script element and {{ suprnova::url::root() }}//evil.example/x each exit non-zero with a widget.html:1: [view- finding naming the rule and write nothing under templates/acme-ui, while a plain view and {{ suprnova::url::root() }}/x install byte for byte; a plan that ignored findings or a scanner that admitted these would fail it.

## Paths not to read
These hold credentials or are excluded from every model by the settings. Do not open them; any file matching one of the patterns is excluded too, tracked or not.
pattern **/.env
pattern **/.env.*
pattern **/*.pem
pattern **/*.p12
pattern **/*.pfx
pattern **/*.key
pattern **/id_rsa
pattern **/id_dsa
pattern **/id_ecdsa
pattern **/id_ed25519
pattern **/id_ecdsa_sk
pattern **/id_ed25519_sk
credential app/.env
credential suprnova-cli/src/templates/files/api/.env.tpl

## Report
Write your report as one JSON file outside the repository and give back its path; it goes to `sudus report` unchanged. It is an object with exactly these fields:
- "attempts": one {"question", "target", "looked_for", "found", "held"} for each pair below, spelled exactly as written. "looked_for" and "found" are text; "held" is true when the work held against what you looked for and false when it did not.
  falsifier BIND-001
  falsifier BIND-002
  falsifier BIND-003
  falsifier BIND-004
  falsifier BIND-005
  falsifier BIND-006
  falsifier BIND-007
  falsifier BIND-008
  falsifier BIND-009
  falsifier BIND-010
  falsifier BIND-011
  falsifier BIND-012
  falsifier BIND-013
  falsifier BIND-014
  falsifier BIND-015
  falsifier IMG-001
  falsifier IMG-002
  falsifier IMG-003
  falsifier IMG-004
  falsifier IMG-005
  falsifier IMG-006
  falsifier IMG-007
  falsifier IMG-008
  falsifier LDB-001
  falsifier LDB-002
  falsifier LDB-003
  falsifier LDB-004
  falsifier LDB-005
  falsifier LDB-006
  falsifier LDB-007
  falsifier LDB-008
  falsifier LDB-009
  falsifier LDB-010
  falsifier LDB-011
  falsifier LDB-012
  falsifier MEM-003
  falsifier PFX-001
  falsifier PFX-002
  falsifier PFX-003
  falsifier PFX-004
  falsifier PFX-005
  falsifier PFX-006
  falsifier PFX-007
  falsifier PFX-008
  falsifier PFX-009
  falsifier PFX-010
  falsifier PFX-011
  falsifier PFX-012
  falsifier PFX-013
  falsifier REG-031
  security application-port-third-round-final
  logic application-port-third-round-final
  complexity application-port-third-round-final
- "interface_attempts": one {"path", "looked_for", "found", "held"} for each path below, each path a JSON string to copy as it is (none: write [])
- "findings": [{"n": 1, "severity": "Critical", "where": "...", "text": "...", "remedy": null}, ...], numbered from 1 in order, or [] when you found nothing. "severity" is Critical, Major or Minor; "where" is the path and line, record or requirement id; "text" is what is wrong and why; "remedy" is one line of advice or null.
- "sudus_bug": null, or the bug report when you stopped on a Sudus bug; then "attempts", "interface_attempts" and "findings" are [].
